{"parentUuid":"ebccfa24-ec65-4fe1-a8be-0084d91eff0e","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"mcp_instructions_delta","addedNames":["plugin:context7:context7","plugin:serena:serena"],"addedBlocks":["## plugin:context7:context7\nUse this server to fetch current documentation whenever the user asks about a library, framework, SDK, API, CLI tool, or cloud service -- even well-known ones like React, Next.js, Prisma, Express, Tailwind, Django, or Spring Boot. This includes API syntax, configuration, version migration, library-specific debugging, setup instructions, and CLI tool usage. Use even when you think you know the answer -- your training data may not reflect recent changes. Prefer this over web search for library docs.\n\nDo not use for: refactoring, writing scripts from scratch, debugging business logic, code review, or general programming concepts.","## plugin:serena:serena\nCRITICAL: Before starting to work on a coding task, call the `initial_instructions` tool to read the 'Serena Instructions Manual'."],"removedNames":[]},"type":"attachment","uuid":"369f5325-2f43-4c66-a2ef-4e8faeef190e","timestamp":"2026-05-15T07:03:20.958Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"ff7c6871-e690-4fdf-bb47-521c4b06efa1","version":"2.1.138","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"369f5325-2f43-4c66-a2ef-4e8faeef190e","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"skill_listing","content":"- ccs-delegation: Auto-activate CCS CLI delegation for deterministic tasks. Parses user input, auto-selects optimal profile (glm/kimi/custom) from ~/.ccs/config.json, enhances prompts with context, executes via `ccs {profile} -p \"task\"` or `ccs {profile}:continue`, and reports results. Triggers on \"use ccs [task]\" patterns, typo/test/refactor keywords. Excludes complex architecture, security-critical code, performance optimization, breaking changes.\n- attach: Port the Claude Code harness layer from this project to a new project directory. Use when bootstrapping a fresh Claude Code project on the same machine, when peers want to copy the harness, or when verifying layer separation via dry-run. Reads .claude/manifest.yaml; copies harness-tagged artifacts only; excludes stockforge biz + personal layers; generates skeleton CLAUDE.md + manifest at target.\n- crawler-reliability: Build reliable web crawlers for data gathering. Use when implementing scrapers for CafeF, NDH, VietnamBiz, YouTube (yt-dlp), Facebook fanpages, or any external data source. Covers Python/Playwright patterns, retry/backoff, selector robustness, rate limiting, monitoring.\n- ddd-tactical-patterns: Apply DDD tactical patterns (aggregate, entity, value object, repository, domain event, anti-corruption layer) correctly in Python/dataclasses code. Use when designing or implementing domain models, repositories, or cross-bounded-context communication in StockForge's 9 BCs.\n- decompose-work: Split a non-trivial task into deterministic-vs-LLM portions before execution. Use when planning multi-step work where some sub-tasks are mechanical (file ops, regex, count, schema validation) and others require judgment (synthesis, design, ambiguity resolution). Outputs structured plan that maps each portion to the cheapest competent tool. Pairs with capability-map.md (model × effort × task_class grounding) and try-n-approaches (S9 — Karpathy autoresearch loop).\n- empirical-probe-first: Probe ALL viable strategies in a multi-option ladder BEFORE committing to one. Use when a session-plan / decision-ADR / spec presents ≥3 strategy options (e.g. \"Strategy A1/A2/A3\" or \"alternative B/C/D\") and the recommended option's source-evidence may be stale. Outputs a probe matrix per strategy + commit decision with empirical citation. Pairs with `vendor-api-probe.sh` (deterministic detection layer; SessionStart hook) and `decompose-work` (deterministic-vs-LLM split per probe). Charter promotion target — codifies L-S32-1 doctrine surfaced at S32 Track A pivot (master-plan recommended A2 vnstock alternate; empirical probe rejected A1+A2; A3 SSI direct httpx adopted instead).\n- evidence-extraction: Extract structured claims from unstructured sources with citation integrity. Use when building extraction pipelines, parsing news articles or KOL transcripts, or anywhere claims are created from raw data. Ensures every claim has source_url, extracted_at, confidence, and verified attribution. Enforces no-LLM-math invariant (I-S1).\n- fastapi-module: Implement FastAPI routers following StockForge conventions. Use when creating a new HTTP endpoint, adding routes, wiring dependency injection, configuring Pydantic DTOs. Covers router boundaries, provider patterns, testing setup. Phase 2+ only — Phase 1 uses Streamlit dashboard directly.\n- grill-maximization: Doctrine for bundling Q&A questions to the human at maximum density per turn (15-20 target, max 25), so each human-in-the-loop touchpoint is information-rich. Use when composing a Q&A bundle for human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/, or when deciding whether to ask one question or wait and bundle. Built on the principle that human-in-the-loop time is the scarcest resource in autonomous coding.\n- hook-diagnostics: Inspect hook event lifecycle (state machine + transcript cache + aggregator output) when triaging failed/stuck hooks or auditing self-awareness rollups. Use during sandwich-verifier review, drift investigation, or post-mortem when component-telemetry.jsonl + dispatch.jsonl + sessions-rollup.tsv need cross-checking.\n- obsidian-vault: Search, create, and manage notes in the StockForge Obsidian vault with wikilinks, frontmatter, and index maintenance. Use when adding content to the knowledge base, navigating entities/concepts, or maintaining the raw/wiki separation. Adapted from mattpocock/skills, MIT licensed. Extended with raw/wiki pattern from Karpathy LLM Wiki.\n- postgres-pgvector: Design Postgres schemas with pgvector and TimescaleDB for StockForge. Use when creating migrations, designing tables, writing queries. Covers vector embeddings for semantic search on Vietnamese text, time-series for price/sentiment data, JSONB vs structured columns.\n- promote-rule: Cluster agent-notes.md entries by lexical similarity, propose promotion of recurring rules to deterministic hook (priority 1), skill (priority 2), or charter (priority 3). Use when agent-notes has accumulated ≥10 new rules since last promotion run, or on-demand at phase boundary. Outputs proposal markdown to observations/. Pairs with capability-map.md (writes new task_class entries) and decompose-work (consumes promotion outcomes).\n- prompt-engineering: Design and optimize LLM prompts for StockForge pipeline stages. Use when writing or refining prompts for claim extraction, KOL recommendation extraction, sentiment analysis, thesis synthesis. Covers context assembly, prompt caching strategy, budget-aware prompts, structured output. Enforces no-LLM-math constraint.\n- qa-escalation: File-based Q&A protocol — composes bundle file in human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/, tracks lifecycle (pending → answered → stale → processed), creates URGENT notification when needed. Use when intent classifier recommends OPEN_QA_BUNDLE / ESCALATE_HUMAN, or grill-maximization has a bundle ready to write. Pairs with grill-maximization (composes content) + Track 5 hooks (auto-move stale).\n- session-memory-l0-l1: Extract structured memories (L0 quick metadata + L1 deep) from a Claude Code session JSONL transcript. L0 is pure regex (file paths, commands, errors, failures incl. Vietnamese phrases, next step). L1 ships the prompt builder + response parser + 15+35 head/tail windowing — caller dispatches the LLM (no auto-invoke in Phase 0). Plus cleanText harness-stripper + TranscriptCache (mtime+size dual-check + LRU). Use when ingesting a session for memory promotion, computing what files/commands/failures the session touched, or preparing an offline LLM extraction.\n- spec-dual-layer: Write specifications with dual-layer structure (business narrative + agent contract). Use when authoring new specs, refining existing specs, or understanding the spec template. Ensures both human stakeholder needs and agent execution needs are served.\n- spec-to-wiki: Convert raw specifications to Obsidian wiki format with wikilinks, frontmatter, and index updates. Use when adding a new spec to the knowledge base, or when regenerating wiki view after spec changes. Maintains raw as source of truth, wiki as navigable view.\n- sync-pull: On-demand confidence-score lookup before a SCOPE+/ARCH/IMPL decision. Reads agent-workspace/memory/sync-tracker/state.tsv + weights.yaml; surfaces the relevant category's score, tier, must-grill flag, and the decision-class threshold. Output drives \"self-decide vs grill\" choice. Use whenever an agent is about to commit a non-trivial decision and needs to ground confidence in historical hit rate per Charter Principle 8 (Calibration over confidence).\n- test-pyramid-balance: Balance unit, integration, and E2E tests properly in Python/pytest. Use when deciding what test to write, reviewing test coverage, or rebalancing skewed test suite. Covers test pyramid shape, BDD vs unit tests, when to use what. Includes stock-domain-specific test categories.\n- try-n-approaches: Karpathy autoresearch outer-loop framing — given a task or signal, generate ≥3 distinct approaches (DEEPEN / BROADEN / ABANDON families), pick one per cheap-first ordering, attach a deterministic metric function (per L-S12-1), and write the experiment frame to learning-data/loop/. Use when an open question surfaces from drift / dogfood / sync-grilling and you need to commit a measurable next step. Pairs with decompose-work (deterministic-vs-LLM split per approach) + capability-map (model × effort grounding) + promote-rule (consumes outcomes).\n- ubiquitous-language: Extract a DDD-style ubiquitous language glossary from the current conversation or domain area. Use when starting work on a new bounded context, when new domain terms emerge, or when verifying consistency between business language and code. Adapted from mattpocock/skills, MIT licensed.\n- user-prompt-intake: Hybrid intent classifier — main session lite-detects trivial prompts, dispatches intent-classifier subagent for complex ones. Use whenever a new user prompt lands (chat or file in human-workspace/user_prompt/) and you need to route the work. Built for full-autonomous loop where every prompt must be classified before action.\n- write-a-skill: Create new Claude Code skills with proper structure, progressive disclosure, and discoverability. Use when extracting a pattern from multiple sessions into a reusable skill, or when user asks to formalize a technique. Adapted from mattpocock/skills, MIT licensed.\n- ccs:continue: Continue last CCS delegation session [AUTO ENHANCE]\n- ccs: Delegate task with intelligent profile selection [AUTO ENHANCE]\n- autonomous: /autonomous — Toggle Autonomous Mode (on/off/status)\n- block: /block — Human-Gate Control (status / clear / raise)\n- budget-check: /budget-check — Current Session Token Budget Status\n- devils-advocate: /devils-advocate — Adversarial Critique\n- drift-check: /drift-check — Run Drift Signals DR1-DR12\n- drill-me: /drill-me — Interactive Ubiquitous Language Extraction\n- grill-me: /grill-me — Relentless Plan/Design Interview\n- handoff-read: /handoff-read — Lightweight Last Session Load\n- master-plan: /master-plan — High-Level Work Decomposition\n- session-end: /session-end — Close Work Session\n- session-start: /session-start — Begin Work Session\n- session-verify: /session-verify — Mid-Session Alignment Check\n- spec-author: /spec-author — Create Dual-Layer Specification\n- spec-to-wiki: /spec-to-wiki — Convert Raw Spec to Obsidian Wiki Format\n- ul-audit: /ul-audit — Audit Ubiquitous Language Consistency\n- vbw-check: /vbw-check — Apply Verify-Before-Write Protocol\n- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch","skillCount":52,"isInitial":true},"type":"attachment","uuid":"cf41ac24-fe69-4648-9f8d-ae5732cfc555","timestamp":"2026-05-15T07:03:20.958Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"ff7c6871-e690-4fdf-bb47-521c4b06efa1","version":"2.1.138","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"fc54414a-fe4d-4a87-8bf0-5d2c6f88b5ea","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"nested_memory","path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\CLAUDE.md","content":{"path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\CLAUDE.md","type":"Project","content":"# agent-workspace/ — Contract\r\n\r\n> **Audience**: agents working inside `agent-workspace/`.\r\n> **Established**: Decision 002 § Track 1 (Workspace Dualism Foundation).\r\n\r\n## Identity\r\n\r\n`agent-workspace/` is the **agent-owned execution + memory** layer. Everything here represents **agent state, decisions, plans, observations, learning**. Agents WRITE here freely (within constitution rules); humans READ here but normally do not edit.\r\n\r\nThis separation exists to prevent two failure modes from orch:\r\n1. **Charter drift via shared-workspace mutation** (orch CF-DOGFOOD-2)\r\n2. **Human cognitive overload from dense session logs** (UP02 §1.3 — \"human đối mặt hàng chục spec/document quá tải nếu không sắp xếp khoa học\")\r\n\r\nThe agent owns this directory but must keep it AUDITABLE — every artifact provenance-linked, every decision sourced.\r\n\r\n---\r\n\r\n## Subdirectories\r\n\r\n| Path | Purpose | Lifecycle |\r\n|---|---|---|\r\n| `constitution/` | **IMMUTABLE without explicit user approval**. Charter-tier rules (autonomous-protocol, model-routing, identity-scope, etc.). Track 7 produces drafts in `proposals/`; user explicit approve to move here. | Edit denied by .claude/settings.json; Write only via human approval gate |\r\n| `memory/project.md` | High-level project state; phase tracker; ADRs (last 5). Update on phase boundaries + architectural decisions. | Long-lived; trim to most recent decisions |\r\n| `memory/current-execution.md` | **THE single routing source of truth**. Active phase + active session + autonomous_mode flag + routing table. Read FIRST every session. | Long-lived; updated each session boundary |\r\n| `memory/agent-notes.md` | Learned rules from real experience (post-mortem / drift / user correction). Append-only mostly. | Long-lived |\r\n| `memory/decisions/` | Sequential ADRs (`NNN-<slug>.md`) + `_template.md` + `README.md` index. 12-field schema (Track 2). | Append-only; supersession via status field |\r\n| `memory/sessions/` | One file per session: `YYYY-MM-DD-session-N.md`. What happened, decisions made, files touched. | Append-only |\r\n| `memory/observations/` | Subagent return artifacts (per Track 6 spec). Each subagent writes one observation file describing what it did. | Append; cleaned periodically by aggregator |\r\n| `memory/checkpoints/` | Session handoff state for self-reboot (Track 5). `latest.md` is canonical pointer; timestamped historical files retained. | Append + latest.md updated each checkpoint |\r\n| `memory/patterns-discovered/` | Pattern mining outputs (Track 0 — DONE) + ongoing pattern catalog. | Append; significant patterns promoted to constitution |\r\n| `memory/drift-logs/` | Drift-check (DR1-DR12) results; runs on hook + on-demand. | Time-series append |\r\n| `memory/post-mortems/` | After significant failure or thesis-revoked event: what failed, root cause, prevention rule. | Append |\r\n| `memory/thesis-log/` | Stock-domain thesis exploration entries. Read-only for IMPL sessions. | Append + revisited per calibration |\r\n| `memory/sync-tracker/` | Track 8a output: Confidence Score SQLite + weights.yaml + auto-rendered `_index.md`. | Live updated by hooks |\r\n| `memory/self-awareness/` | Track 9 output: model × effort × thinking profile cards + known-issues + best-practices. | Live updated by Stop-hook aggregator |\r\n| `memory/mistake-log.md` | Track 7 deliverable: structured failure catalog. What went wrong / Root cause / Prevention rule / Severity. | Append; pre-flight read by all agents |\r\n| `session-plans/pending/` | Plans authored, awaiting execution. Phase 0 plan lives here. | Move to `completed/` when done |\r\n| `session-plans/completed/` | Executed plans; archived for reference + retrospective input. | Append |\r\n| `quality-reports/{deterministic,probabilistic,drift-reports}/` | Tier-1/2/3 gate outputs (CLAUDE.md § Quality Gates). | Per-run files |\r\n| `ubiquitous-language/` | DDD glossary + domain-mapping + drift-log. Stockforge stock-domain vocabulary. | Append; canonical via `/drill-me` |\r\n| `calibration/` | Empirical hit-rate data per signal type. Confidence claims MUST trace here (CLAUDE.md hard rule). | Append per thesis post-mortem |\r\n| `research/` | Research notes from `research-scanner` subagent dispatches (e.g., reference-repo studies). | Append |\r\n\r\n---\r\n\r\n## Contract Rules (BINDING)\r\n\r\n1. **Constitution is immutable absent explicit human approval.** `Edit/Write` to `agent-workspace/constitution/**` is in `.claude/settings.json` deny list. Track 7 writes constitution drafts to `proposals/`; user explicit approve moves them.\r\n\r\n2. **`memory/decisions/` is append-mostly + supersession-status.** Don't delete decisions; mark `status: superseded-by-D-NNN` to revoke. Sequential numbering (`001`, `002`, ...) — don't reuse numbers.\r\n\r\n3. **`current-execution.md` is the routing source-of-truth.** No skill, agent, or command may hardcode phase paths or session paths from memory; resolve via this file. Stale paths in CLAUDE.md or skills = anti-pattern.\r\n\r\n4. **Never edit `obsidian-vault/raw/`.** Immutable. Vault writes only to `wiki/`. (Established in stockforge CLAUDE.md.)\r\n\r\n5. **Provenance is mandatory.** Every decision file references its source (human prompt / spec clause / drift detection / agent inference). Every thesis cites source + as-of date (I-S2). Every confidence claim cites historical hit rate (I-S20-equivalent).\r\n\r\n6. **Agents MAY `git commit`; agents MUST NOT `git push`.** Pushing to remote is human-only. (Updated 2026-05-15 per project-owner directive; see `memory/decisions/060-*`.)\r\n\r\n7. **`patterns-discovered/` artifacts are READ-ONLY for production code.** Patterns must be promoted to constitution / skills / hook scripts before becoming binding. (Otherwise they're informal notes, not contracts.)\r\n\r\n---\r\n\r\n## Reading Priority for Agent\r\n\r\nPer `agent-workspace/constitution/autonomous-protocol.md` (Track 7) and stockforge `CLAUDE.md` § Session Protocol:\r\n\r\n1. **`memory/current-execution.md`** — first; resolve active track\r\n2. **`memory/project.md`** — project state\r\n3. **`memory/checkpoints/latest.md`** if a recent checkpoint exists (within last 24h) — resume context\r\n4. **`memory/agent-notes.md`** — learned rules\r\n5. **`memory/mistake-log.md`** — pre-flight failure catalog\r\n6. **Last 3 files in `memory/sessions/`** — recent context\r\n7. **`session-plans/pending/<active-plan>.md`** — current plan\r\n8. **Relevant constitution files** as task demands (don't load all every session)\r\n9. **Relevant skills + agents + commands** as task demands\r\n\r\n---\r\n\r\n## Anti-Patterns to Avoid\r\n\r\n(From `memory/patterns-discovered/SYNTHESIS.md` § 6, full catalog 23 items. Highest-priority for this directory:)\r\n\r\n- **AP-1 Same-agent self-review** — never review your own implementation; dispatch fresh-context verifier\r\n- **AP-2 Self-track wind-down** — `.transcript-tokens` real-transcript is authoritative, not LLM self-track\r\n- **AP-5 Charter-coherence defer overriding user-CRITICAL** — re-read all `user_prompt/*` at every phase entry; never silently defer USER-CRITICAL\r\n- **AP-7 Performative SC ticking** — defer with explicit prerequisites > fire vacuous proposals\r\n- **AP-8 Pre-staged work causing checkpoint drift** — update `current-execution.md` immediately on task complete, not session-end\r\n- **AP-17 Identity drift** — stockforge is AI-first VN stock advisory; not generic financial framework (see `constitution/identity-scope.md` once Track 7 ships)\r\n- **AP-23 Continuous LLM-Guardian** — deterministic hooks = Guardian; LLM Guardian only at session-end aggregation\r\n\r\n---\r\n\r\n## Connection to human-workspace/\r\n\r\nThis directory is the agent's domain. Human's domain is `human-workspace/`. {"parentUuid":"f7e6de4a-c664-4ed4-a6b2-2acb5125d6f5","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"nested_memory","path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\human-workspace\\CLAUDE.md","content":{"path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\human-workspace\\CLAUDE.md","type":"Project","content":"# human-workspace/ — Contract\r\n\r\n> **Audience**: agents reading anything inside `human-workspace/`.\r\n> **Established**: Decision 002 § Track 1 (Workspace Dualism Foundation).\r\n\r\n## Identity\r\n\r\n`human-workspace/` is the **human-authored authority** layer. Everything here represents **human decisions, scope, intent, and verification**. Agents READ this directory as authoritative; agents do NOT WRITE here except in one designated channel (`q-and-a/pending/` — where agents post questions for human to answer).\r\n\r\nThis separation exists because of a failure mode observed in the orch sister project (orch `decisions/040`, post-mortem CF-DOGFOOD-2): when human and agent share a single workspace without contract, agent silently overrides human-CRITICAL items via technical-defer logic. The user explicitly raised this concern in `human-workspace/user_prompt/20260429_02_init.txt` §1.1.\r\n\r\n---\r\n\r\n## Subdirectories\r\n\r\n| Path | Purpose | Who writes |\r\n|---|---|---|\r\n| `user_prompt/` | Human's mid-flight scope/idea drops. Naming: `YYYYMMDD_NN_<slug>.txt`. Files are immutable post-creation (audit trail). | **Human only**. Agent: read-only. |\r\n| `decisions/` | Human's strategic decisions (e.g., scope-lock, charter-revision-trigger, peer-share authorization). Format: `D-H-<NNN>-<slug>.md`. | **Human only**. Agent: read-only. |\r\n| `q-and-a/pending/` | **The ONE channel where agent writes inside human-workspace**. Agent drops Q&A bundles per Grill Maximization doctrine; human responds inline or moves to `answered/`. | **Both** (agent writes new bundles; human writes answers). |\r\n| `q-and-a/answered/` | Q&A bundles human has answered (with answers inline). Agent reads answers and acts. Archive forever; provenance trail. | **Human moves** (drag from `pending/`); agent reads. |\r\n| `q-and-a/stale/` | Pending bundles that exceeded their `expected_answer_by` (default 24h, urgent 4h). Auto-moved by SessionStart hook (Track 5). | **Hook moves**; both read. |\r\n| `notifications/` | Agent push to human — summaries, alerts, milestones. Format: `N-<TS>-<level>-<slug>.md`. Levels: INFO / SUMMARY / ALERT. | **Agent writes**; human reads. |\r\n\r\n---\r\n\r\n## Contract Rules (BINDING)\r\n\r\n1. **Agent NEVER writes to `user_prompt/` or `decisions/`.** Permission deny. If something feels like a human decision but the human hasn't written it, dispatch a Q&A bundle into `q-and-a/pending/` instead.\r\n\r\n2. **`user_prompt/` files are immutable post-creation.** Agent never deletes, renames, or edits these files. They are the audit trail of human interventions.\r\n\r\n3. **Q&A bundle format** (per Track 4 spec): YAML frontmatter (`id`, `topic`, `opened_at`, `expected_answer_by`, `priority`, `related_decisions`, `status`) + question body + answer-fill section. Each bundle = up to 25 questions per Grill Maximization doctrine; split if more.\r\n\r\n4. **Agent does not move files between `q-and-a/{pending,answered,stale}/`** — that's human's role (or hook's, in case of stale). Agent only WRITES new pending bundles and READS answers.\r\n\r\n5. **Notifications are read-only for human.** Human shouldn't edit `notifications/*` files; if disagreement with notification content, drop a `user_prompt/` instead. Notifications are the agent's account of events.\r\n\r\n6. **Provenance pointers**: every `agent-workspace/memory/decisions/D-<NNN>` should reference its source `human-workspace/user_prompt/<file>` or `human-workspace/decisions/D-H-<NNN>` if the agent's decision derives from human input.\r\n\r\n7. **No multi-tenancy in this directory.** Per identity-scope.md (Track 7), stockforge is single-tenant Phase 0-5; if peer share happens it's via git-fork, each peer has their own `human-workspace/` per fork.\r\n\r\n---\r\n\r\n## Reading Priority for Agent\r\n\r\nWhen loading session context (per `agent-workspace/constitution/autonomous-protocol.md` Track 7):\r\n\r\n1. **All `user_prompt/*.txt`** — every prompt, not just most recent. Per orch lesson (decision-040 USER-CRITICAL tier): never silently defer human-CRITICAL items.\r\n2. **All `decisions/*.md`** — human's strategic decisions are above charter principles in priority; charter is below human (charter was authored by human in the first place).\r\n3. **Latest `q-and-a/answered/*`** — recent answers update agent's understanding.\r\n4. **Open `q-and-a/pending/*`** — flag if waiting on human answers.\r\n5. **Recent `notifications/*`** (last 7 days) — for situational awareness.\r\n\r\n---\r\n\r\n## Agent Self-Check\r\n\r\nBefore writing to `human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/`, ask:\r\n- Is this question genuinely something the human must decide? (Charter principle / Charter-affecting / SCOPE-affecting / above ARCH-tier confidence threshold)\r\n- Have I already grilled this in a recent bundle? (Don't ask twice; don't pollute pending/)\r\n- Am I bundling per Grill Maximization (target 15-20 questions, max 25)? Each rule earned through real experience (post-mortem, drift detection, user correction).\n4\t> Rules here have higher status than convention but lower than constitution.\n5\t\n6\t## Rule Format\n7\t\n8\tUse this template:\n9\t\n10\t```markdown\n11\t### YYYY-MM-DD: [Short rule name]\n12\t**Context**: What situation triggered learning this\n13\t**Rule**: The actionable rule\n14\t**Anti-example**: What was done wrong\n15\t**Correct example**: What should be done instead\n16\t**Severity**: critical | high | medium | low\n17\t**Auto-detect**: yes/no — can a drift signal catch this?\n18\t```\n19\t\n20\t---\n21\t\n22\t## Recent Rules (digest; last 5)\n23\t\n24\t### 2026-05-14 (S318): Anti-pattern-class fixes need exhaustive instance-grep + consumer-trace BEFORE impl — L-S318-1\n25\t**Context**: S318 converted notification-writing hooks from per-fire-timestamp filenames to fixed-name idempotent (notification-spam generation root fix). Initial scope analysis anchored on the S317 checkpoint's named-6 spam hooks + a same-class scan (→9), then implemented. The fresh-context sandwich-verifier (AP-1) found a 10th instance — `essential-routing-fields-verifier.sh`, the exact date-bucket twin of `working-memory-budget-audit.sh` which I HAD converted (inconsistent). Separately, a mid-session due-diligence grep found `guardian-output-inspect-first.sh` was a *consumer* globbing the old `*-bash-hook-lint-warn.md` pattern — my fixed-name change would have silently broken it on ship.\n26\t**Rule**: When fixing an anti-pattern CLASS (not a single bug), BEFORE writing any edit: (1) grep the anti-pattern signature exhaustively across the whole target tree — enumerate EVERY instance; never treat a checkpoint's / plan's named subset as the complete set. (2) For any change that renames or alters an interface (filename, env var, log path, schema), grep for every CONSUMER of the old form across all file types. Only then implement. The writer/producer side is the obvious half; the consumer side and the \"did I get them all\" completeness check are the half that gets missed.\n27\t**Anti-example**: S318 implemented 9 writer fixes anchored on the checkpoint's list, shipped, then the verifier + a late grep surfaced a 10th writer + a broken consumer.\n28\t**Correct example**: Up front — `grep` the per-fire-timestamp notification pattern across `scripts/hooks/`, AND `grep` every notification slug across the whole repo for consumers — then implement the full set in one pass.\n29\t**Severity**: low (S318 caught + fixed both in-session; no broken ship). The cost is a slower, churned session, not a production defect.\n30\t**Auto-detect**: partial — interface-rename consumer-breakage is detectable: a `rename-consumer-lint` could flag when a hook's notification/log filename literal changes in a diff while grep still finds `*-<oldslug>` globs elsewhere. Promote-to-hook candidate if a 2nd consumer-breakage instance surfaces.\n31\t\n32\t### 2026-05-14 (S312): Single-shot escalation pattern + age-proxy timestamp + plan-text/impl divergence — L-S312-1/2/3\n33\t**Context**: S312 sandwich-verifier adversarial review (AP-1 fresh-context) of W0-1 FSM IMPL surfaced 2 IMPORTANT design defects beyond functional acceptance:\n34\t  (a) **L-S312-1 (single-shot escalation)**: severity-classifier atomically rewrites `.severity-state.tsv` each Stop; orphan-detector emits HIGH only at IN_FLIGHT→ORPHANED transition. Result: row gets ONE escalation attempt. If user dismisses or misses the AskUserQuestion, orphan goes silent forever — recreating the exact slip pattern that caused L-S258-2 (4 instances). Verifier proved empirically: row 6h post-orphan-transition produces zero new HIGH severity rows on subsequent Stops.\n35\t  (b) **L-S312-3 (age proxy from immutable timestamp)**: TSV schema uses `detected_ts` (col1, written-once) as age reference for orphan detection. Once future writers transition state, the stale `detected_ts` falsely triggers orphan-flag within minutes of OBSERVATION_WRITTEN transition.\n36\t  (c) **L-S312-2 (plan-text vs impl-behavior divergence)**: Plan said D3.2 close-verifier \"refuses close\" on OBSERVATION_WRITTEN; impl emits WARN+notification with RC=0 (best-effort, doesn't block). Words \"refuse\" vs \"warn\" in sandwich-architect/dev contract should be precise.\n37\t**Rule**:\n38\t  (1) **Hooks emitting severity-state rows MUST consider re-emit cadence**, not just first-detection: write per-artifact marker file `agent-workspace/memory/.<emission-class>-<sha-of-artifact>.last`; re-emit if marker mtime > threshold (default 6h / 24h / 72h / 7d / monthly-CRITICAL-escalation).\n39\t  (2) **TSV schemas tracking lifecycle state SHOULD include `last_transition_at` column** as age reference, NOT use `detected_ts` (which is write-once). Alternative: explicit writer-contract that state mutation MUST refresh col1. Always prefer (a) col7 last_transition_at to avoid writer-contract leakage.\n40\t  (3) **Plan-text language MUST distinguish hard-refuse vs soft-warn**: Stop hooks default to best-effort RC=0; if plan says \"refuse close\" the author MUST either explicitly mark the hook as blocking-tier (e.g., StopFailure event, or non-Stop event-type) OR amend wording to \"warn on close attempt + log to notifications\".\n41\t**Anti-example**: W0-1 (S311) shipped functionally correct but with single-shot escalation gap → re-creates L-S258-2 slip pattern.\n42\t**Correct example**: W0-1b (S313) addresses via per-artifact marker + col7 schema extension.\n43\t**Severity**: high (single-shot escalation directly undermines L-S258-2 closure claim; age-proxy is dormant defect that activates with W0-2/3/4)\n44\t**Auto-detect**: yes — `re-emit-cadence-lint.sh` Stop hook can scan `scripts/hooks/*` for `emit_row` calls without companion marker-check; promote-to-hook after 1+ recurrence.\n45\t\n46\t### 2026-05-14 (S310): Unified severity + escalation + block + Telegram-skeleton system SHIPPED — L-S310-2\n47\t**Context**: User-surfaced 7 harness gaps after audit (fragmented severity schemas across Q&A/decision/mistake/notification; no BLOCK mechanism; 48h-only stale escalation; no proactive AskUserQuestion auto-fire; no external channel; orphan notification accumulation; fragmented watchdog hooks with no central severity router). CRITICAL=stale-checkpoint marker / Q&A age≥96h / charter-violation marker / ghost-greening marker / mistake-log severity=critical. HIGH=Q&A age≥6h pending / charter-tier ADR PROPOSED age≥24h cool-down / mistake-log severity=high / notification ALERT-URGENT keyword. MEDIUM=ARCH/SCOPE PROPOSED age≥12h / notification WARN. LOW=below threshold / log-only. Filters: skip status=answered/closed/resolved bundles; skip _template.md+README.md+TEMPLATE.md+index.md; skip urgent.md+digest-*.md (self-loop prevention).\n50\t  (2) **escalation-engine.sh** (Stop + SessionStart + UserPromptSubmit; arg $1=event): reads .severity-state.tsv + acts per row. CRITICAL → writes `.autonomous-BLOCKED` flag (idempotent presence-check) + appends URGENT entry to urgent.md + Telegram push. HIGH (hour-bucket idempotent) → appends URGENT entry + UserPromptSubmit stdout injection demanding agent fire AskUserQuestion + Telegram push. MEDIUM (week-bucket idempotent) → weekly digest-<YYYY-Www>.md. LOW → log only.\n51\t  (3) **autonomous-block-enforcer.sh** (UserPromptSubmit FIRST chain + PreToolUse FIRST chain; arg $1=event): when `.autonomous-BLOCKED` flag present, UserPromptSubmit emits loud BLOCKED context via stdout (visible to LLM as additionalContext); PreToolUse exits RC=2 (deny) for Edit/Write/Bash/MultiEdit/NotebookEdit/Agent; Read/Glob/Grep allowed for diagnostic. Override: `STOCKFORGE_FORCE_AUTONOMOUS=1` env bypasses (logged).\n52\t  (4) **telegram-push.sh** (called by escalation-engine for CRITICAL/HIGH only; SKELETON): reads `$STOCKFORGE_TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN` + `$STOCKFORGE_TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID` env from `.claude/settings.local.json` (gitignored per wildcard-permissions-preference). User must (a) create bot via @BotFather, (b) extract chat_id via getUpdates, (c) set both env vars. Without creds → silent no-op. Idempotency: SHA-keyed per-hour marker. DRY_RUN mode for testing without live API.\n53\t**Anti-example**: S259→S309 (50 sessions ~20h) running sync-grilling/idle book-keeping while Q-INT mega-bundle sat with `askuserquestion_fired: false` (file-based bundle silent). No deterministic hook fired escalation. No block mechanism. urgent.md ignored.\n54\t**Correct example**: This session (S310) — applied L-S310-1 ritual demotion + L-S310-2 severity system. Live run detected 3 legitimate HIGH items (D-034 charter v1.1 PROPOSED 170h / mistake-log.md severity=high / D-053-empirical-divergence notification 18h) and 0 CRITICAL. urgent.md auto-populated by escalation-engine.\n55\t**Severity**: high\n56\t**Auto-detect**: yes — system IS the auto-detector. Self-test via 4 firing-tests (`severity-classifier-fire-test.sh` 5 TC / `escalation-engine-fire-test.sh` 7 TC / `autonomous-block-enforcer-fire-test.sh` 11 TC / `telegram-push-fire-test.sh` 3 TC = 26 TC total). Telegram E2E verification gated on user config.\n57\t**Deferred (separate session)**: Phase E — migrate existing watchdog hooks (qa-stale-urgent-escalator 48h→deprecate / idle-escape-detector / phase-status-coherence / harness-health-* / budget-watchdog) to emit rows into .severity-state.tsv instead of/in addition to ad-hoc urgent.md writes. Tracking: `agent-workspace/proposals/harness-severity-escalation-system-2026-05-14.md` §3 Phase E.\n58\t\n59\t### 2026-05-14 (S310): Ritual demotion BINDING — sync-grilling cadence + routine-idle close — L-S310-1\n60\t**Context**: User-surfaced harness gap. 40-session audit (S270-S309) revealed 0% product work; 62.5% routine-idle close ritual (24 consecutive idle closes, catch-rate 0/24); 35% sync-grilling mechanical cadence (18 fires, catch-rate 0/18, SCOPE drift +5pt purely from cadence). Both rituals exceed CLAUDE.md ritual-demotion threshold (3+ sessions catch=0) by 8× / 6×. Root cause: Q-INT mega-bundle filed S259 sat silent 20h with `askuserquestion_fired: false` while loop ran busy-work; agent waited for \"user-readiness signal\" that never came. User directive \"fix đi chứ?\" upgraded proposal from PROPOSED → BINDING.\n61\t**Rule**:\n62\t  (1) DO NOT invoke `scripts/hooks/sync-grilling-call.sh` on 3-session cadence. UP-06 \"NO-Silent-Default\" applies to USER-FACING side too — agent cannot silently wait beyond 24h.\n65\t**Anti-example**: S259→S309 (50 sessions, ~20h wall-clock) running sync-grilling cadence + idle book-keeping while Q-INT mega-bundle sat with `askuserquestion_fired: false`.\n66\t**Correct example**: This session (S310) — applied demotion + fired AskUserQuestion to surface blockers + emitted one-line state ack instead of busy-work close ritual.\n67\t**Severity**: high\n68\t**Auto-detect**: yes — add `ritual-catch-rate-watchdog.sh` Stop hook to count consecutive sync-grilling fires with reason text matching \"zero NEW SCOPE-tier divergence\" / consecutive ROUTINE-IDLE sessions writing only the 4-artifact set; ALERT at N=3, hard-block at N=5.\n69\t\n70\t### 2026-05-11 (S248): AP-23 \"harness-firing-test-gap\" class consolidated under deterministic hook — L-S247-1\n71\t**Context**: 6 AP-23 instances accumulated (M-S189-1, S243 lock-trap, S245 lock-pid-secondary, S245 env-prefix Form-A, S245 grep-multi-S-token, S247 env-prefix Form-B). Root cause shared across all 6: firing-test authors modeled dev-shell invocation instead of actual Windows Claude Code hook-executor spawn topology.\n72\t**Rule**: `scripts/hooks/firing-test-spawn-context-lint.sh` (L-S247-1 promoted hook) now enforces three deterministic passes at every Stop event:\n73\t  (1) Form-B env-wrap detection: any `\"command\": \"env VAR=val bash ...\"` in settings.json → WARN (sibling `settings-inline-env-prefix-detector.sh` covers Form-A)\n74\t  (2) bash-c / stdin-redirect hooks missing companion firing-test → WARN\n75\t  (3) bash-c / stdin-redirect hooks whose companion firing-test lacks `# SPAWN-CONTEXT:` marker → WARN\n76\t  Form-C (positional-arg) is safe in Windows executor — requires firing-test but NOT SPAWN-CONTEXT marker.\n77\t**Anti-example** (all 6 prior instances): firing-test invoked hook via `bash \"$HOOK\"` in dev-shell context — did not exercise env-var absence, PPID=1, or Windows PE32+ executor path.\n78\t**Correct example**: For bash-c/stdin-redirect hooks, include in firing-test: `# SPAWN-CONTEXT: bash-c` block documenting that the fixture exercises the actual spawn topology, NOT dev-shell shortcut.\n79\t**Severity**: high. **Auto-detect**: yes — `firing-test-spawn-context-lint.sh` is the auto-detector (WARN-only; promote to BLOCKING after 5 clean sessions per S99 ritual-demotion mirror).\n80\t**Coverage of prior instances**: All 6 prior instances are now covered by this hook. No additional inline rules needed.\n81\t\n82\t### 2026-05-10 (S246): Task-tool subagent runs in-process; does NOT fire SessionStart hooks — L-S246-B\n83\t**Context**: S246-B sandwich-verifier empirical test (Scope 1) at 22:30+07:00 found that Agent-tool subagent dispatch on Windows runs in-process within parent claude.exe — NOT as a separate OS process. `tasklist //FI \"IMAGENAME eq claude.exe\"` showed ONLY the parent (pid 18176). `agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log` had zero SessionStart entries during subagent execution. This is OPPOSITE to my and the architect's prior assumption.\n84\t**Rule**: Task-tool/Agent-tool subagent dispatch DOES NOT fire SessionStart hooks. Only `claude --print` / `claude -p` (e.g., from `subagent_transport.py:subprocess.run`) spawns separate claude.exe processes that fire SessionStart hooks. **These two \"subagent\" mechanisms have entirely different harness implications:**\n85\t  - **Agent-tool**: in-process, no SessionStart, no lock-hook firing, no separate billing path\n86\t  - **claude --print**: separate process, SessionStart fires, lock-hook fires, separate per-call billing\n87\t**Anti-example**: Architect's S246 plan (and my S246-B dispatch) assumed Agent-tool subagent SessionStart would BLOCK — speculative analysis without empirical test. The Strategy (f) hook is therefore production-safe for Agent-tool subagents (BLOCK is unreachable via that path).\n88\t**Correct example**: Verify subagent spawn topology empirically before designing hook compatibility — `tasklist` + `.session-hooks.log` + `lock-rc-probe.sh` are deterministic checks.\n89\t**Why**: Hook design assumed a class of failure that doesn't exist on this platform. Empirical RC discrimination (Strategy f vs e vs c) was correct; the in-process-vs-spawn-process distinction was missed by both PLAN and IMPL but caught by VERIFY.\n90\t**How to apply**: When designing SessionStart-tier hooks that affect autonomous-mode behavior, distinguish (a) Agent-tool subagents (bypass hook chain entirely) from (b) claude-CLI-spawned subagents (fire full hook chain). Only the latter path needs hook-compatibility consideration.\n91\t**Severity**: high (knowledge-correcting). **Auto-detect**: yes — `lock-rc-probe.sh` retained as diagnostic for future spawn-topology verification.\n92\t\n93\t### 2026-05-10 (S245): Two new AP-23-family harness defects — env-prefix-fails-spawn + grep-multi-S-token — L-S245+-2/3\n94\t**Context**: Both surfaced from user-displayed TUI errors mid-S245 turn. (1) `.claude/settings.json` lines 204/208/212/277/281/285 used `CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT=X bash \"...\"` env-prefix syntax — Claude Code's hook spawn layer fails this with `bash: CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT=...: No such file or directory` (3 SessionStart + 3 UserPromptSubmit hooks all silently broken since add; harness-health log last entry 20:43:40 confirms zero production fires post-add). (2) `scripts/hooks/promotion-cycle-trigger.sh:47` `grep -oE 'S[0-9]+'` on basename `promote-rule-S245-L-S240-5-cycle.md` returned TWO matches (S245+S240) → multi-line `LAST_PROMOTE_SESSION` → line 51 `$((..))` syntax error → SESSION_DELTA unbound at line 55.\n95\t**Rule**: (1) When wiring env-vars in Claude Code hook commands, prepend `env` (e.g. `env VAR=val bash \"...\"`) — DO NOT rely on inline POSIX env-prefix syntax which the hook spawn layer mishandles. (2) When parsing session-IDs from `promote-rule-S<N>-*.md` filenames, ALWAYS pipe `grep -oE 'S[0-9]+'` through `| head -1` because filename may contain referenced lesson IDs (`L-S<M>-*`) producing multi-line output that breaks downstream arithmetic.\n96\t**Anti-example** (1): `\"command\": \"CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT=UserPromptSubmit bash \\\"...\\\"\"` — fails at runtime. (2): `LAST_PROMOTE_SESSION=$(basename ... | grep -oE 'S[0-9]+' | tr -d 'S')` — multi-line on `S245-L-S240` filenames.\n97\t**Correct example** (1): `\"command\": \"env CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT=UserPromptSubmit bash \\\"...\\\"\"` — `env` is a real executable that the spawn layer recognizes. (2): `LAST_PROMOTE_SESSION=$(basename ... | grep -oE 'S[0-9]+' | head -1 | tr -d 'S')`.\n98\t**Why**: Both are AP-23 family \"harness ships with verification-time defect because firing-test fixture didn't exercise actual-Windows-spawn-context or actual-filename-edge-case.\" Class instance count: 5+ (M-S189-1, S243 lock-trap, S245 lock-pid, S245 env-prefix, S245 grep-multi-S). No deterministic hook fired escalation. No block mechanism. urgent.md ignored.\n**Correct example**: This session (S310) — applied L-S310-1 ritual demotion + L-S310-2 severity system. Live run detected 3 legitimate HIGH items (D-034 charter v1.1 PROPOSED 170h / mistake-log.md severity=high / D-053-empirical-divergence notification 18h) and 0 CRITICAL. urgent.md auto-populated by escalation-engine.\n**Severity**: high\n**Auto-detect**: yes — system IS the auto-detector. Self-test via 4 firing-tests (`severity-classifier-fire-test.sh` 5 TC / `escalation-engine-fire-test.sh` 7 TC / `autonomous-block-enforcer-fire-test.sh` 11 TC / `telegram-push-fire-test.sh` 3 TC = 26 TC total). Telegram E2E verification gated on user config.\n**Deferred (separate session)**: Phase E — migrate existing watchdog hooks (qa-stale-urgent-escalator 48h→deprecate / idle-escape-detector / phase-status-coherence / harness-health-* / budget-watchdog) to emit rows into .severity-state.tsv instead of/in addition to ad-hoc urgent.md writes. Tracking: `agent-workspace/proposals/harness-severity-escalation-system-2026-05-14.md` §3 Phase E.\n\n### 2026-05-14 (S310): Ritual demotion BINDING — sync-grilling cadence + routine-idle close — L-S310-1\n**Context**: User-surfaced harness gap. 40-session audit (S270-S309) revealed 0% product work; 62.5% routine-idle close ritual (24 consecutive idle closes, catch-rate 0/24); 35% sync-grilling mechanical cadence (18 fires, catch-rate 0/18, SCOPE drift +5pt purely from cadence). Both rituals exceed CLAUDE.md ritual-demotion threshold (3+ sessions catch=0) by 8× / 6×. Root cause: Q-INT mega-bundle filed S259 sat silent 20h with `askuserquestion_fired: false` while loop ran busy-work; agent waited for \"user-readiness signal\" that never came. User directive \"fix đi chứ?\" upgraded proposal from PROPOSED → BINDING.\n**Rule**:\n  (1) DO NOT invoke `scripts/hooks/sync-grilling-call.sh` on 3-session cadence. Authorized triggers only: (a) user ratifies a pending SCOPE+CHARTER bundle producing NEW divergence; (b) charter ratification opens NEW SCOPE-tier signals (e.g., post-D-055 cool-down); (c) Phase boundary entry triggers re-verification; (d) user submits NEW user_prompt with SCOPE-tier directive.\n  (2) DO NOT write ROUTINE-IDLE close artifacts (archive checkpoint, prepend current-execution row, new session log, rewrite latest.md) when entry delta-check returns \"ZERO new actionable signal AND no PRIORITY became unblocked\". Instead emit one-line state ack to user.\n  (3) When SCOPE+CHARTER bundle pending >24h with no user signal AND agent has no unblocked product work, agent MUST fire AskUserQuestion to surface blockers (NOT spin in idle/cadence rituals). UP-06 \"NO-Silent-Default\" applies to USER-FACING side too — agent cannot silently wait beyond 24h.\n**Anti-example**: S259→S309 (50 sessions, ~20h wall-clock) running sync-grilling cadence + idle book-keeping while Q-INT mega-bundle sat with `askuserquestion_fired: false`.\n**Correct example**: This session (S310) — applied demotion + fired AskUserQuestion to surface blockers + emitted one-line state ack instead of busy-work close ritual.\n**Severity**: high\n**Auto-detect**: yes — add `ritual-catch-rate-watchdog.sh` Stop hook to count consecutive sync-grilling fires with reason text matching \"zero NEW SCOPE-tier divergence\" / consecutive ROUTINE-IDLE sessions writing only the 4-artifact set; ALERT at N=3, hard-block at N=5.\n\n### 2026-05-11 (S248): AP-23 \"harness-firing-test-gap\" class consolidated under deterministic hook — L-S247-1\n**Context**: 6 AP-23 instances accumulated (M-S189-1, S243 lock-trap, S245 lock-pid-secondary, S245 env-prefix Form-A, S245 grep-multi-S-token, S247 env-prefix Form-B). All were statically detectable via regex on `.claude/settings.json` + structural lint on companion firing-test fixtures. AP-23 HOOK-promotion threshold (>=3 instances) was overdue by 3 instances. Root cause shared across all 6: firing-test authors modeled dev-shell invocation instead of actual Windows Claude Code hook-executor spawn topology.\n**Rule**: `scripts/hooks/firing-test-spawn-context-lint.sh` (L-S247-1 promoted hook) now enforces three deterministic passes at every Stop event:\n  (1) Form-B env-wrap detection: any `\"command\": \"env VAR=val bash ...\"` in settings.json → WARN (sibling `settings-inline-env-prefix-detector.sh` covers Form-A)\n  (2) bash-c / stdin-redirect hooks missing companion firing-test → WARN\n  (3) bash-c / stdin-redirect hooks whose companion firing-test lacks `# SPAWN-CONTEXT:` marker → WARN\n  Form-C (positional-arg) is safe in Windows executor — requires firing-test but NOT SPAWN-CONTEXT marker.\n**Anti-example** (all 6 prior instances): firing-test invoked hook via `bash \"$HOOK\"` in dev-shell context — did not exercise env-var absence, PPID=1, or Windows PE32+ executor path.\n**Correct example**: For bash-c/stdin-redirect hooks, include in firing-test: `# SPAWN-CONTEXT: bash-c` block documenting that the fixture exercises the actual spawn topology, NOT dev-shell shortcut.\n**Severity**: high. **Auto-detect**: yes — `firing-test-spawn-context-lint.sh` is the auto-detector (WARN-only; promote to BLOCKING after 5 clean sessions per S99 ritual-demotion mirror).\n**Coverage of prior instances**: All 6 prior instances are now covered by this hook. No additional inline rules needed.\n\n### 2026-05-10 (S246): Task-tool subagent runs in-process; does NOT fire SessionStart hooks — L-S246-B\n**Context**: S246-B sandwich-verifier empirical test (Scope 1) at 22:30+07:00 found that Agent-tool subagent dispatch on Windows runs in-process within parent claude.exe — NOT as a separate OS process. `tasklist //FI \"IMAGENAME eq claude.exe\"` showed ONLY the parent (pid 18176). `agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log` had zero SessionStart entries during subagent execution. This is OPPOSITE to my and the architect's prior assumption.\n**Rule**: Task-tool/Agent-tool subagent dispatch DOES NOT fire SessionStart hooks. Only `claude --print` / `claude -p` (e.g., from `subagent_transport.py:subprocess.run`) spawns separate claude.exe processes that fire SessionStart hooks. **These two \"subagent\" mechanisms have entirely different harness implications:**\n  - **Agent-tool**: in-process, no SessionStart, no lock-hook firing, no separate billing path\n  - **claude --print**: separate process, SessionStart fires, lock-hook fires, separate per-call billing\n**Anti-example**: Architect's S246 plan (and my S246-B dispatch) assumed Agent-tool subagent SessionStart would BLOCK — speculative analysis without empirical test. The Strategy (f) hook is therefore production-safe for Agent-tool subagents (BLOCK is unreachable via that path).\n**Correct example**: Verify subagent spawn topology empirically before designing hook compatibility — `tasklist` + `.session-hooks.log` + `lock-rc-probe.sh` are deterministic checks.\n**Why**: Hook design assumed a class of failure that doesn't exist on this platform. Empirical RC discrimination (Strategy f vs e vs c) was correct; the in-process-vs-spawn-process distinction was missed by both PLAN and IMPL but caught by VERIFY.\n**How to apply**: When designing SessionStart-tier hooks that affect autonomous-mode behavior, distinguish (a) Agent-tool subagents (bypass hook chain entirely) from (b) claude-CLI-spawned subagents (fire full hook chain). Only the latter path needs hook-compatibility consideration.\n**Severity**: high (knowledge-correcting). **Auto-detect**: yes — `lock-rc-probe.sh` retained as diagnostic for future spawn-topology verification.\n\n### 2026-05-10 (S245): Two new AP-23-family harness defects — env-prefix-fails-spawn + grep-multi-S-token — L-S245+-2/3\n**Context**: Both surfaced from user-displayed TUI errors mid-S245 turn. (1) `.claude/settings.json` lines 204/208/212/277/281/285 used `CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT=X bash \"...\"` env-prefix syntax — Claude Code's hook spawn layer fails this with `bash: CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT=...: No such file or directory` (3 SessionStart + 3 UserPromptSubmit hooks all silently broken since add; harness-health log last entry 20:43:40 confirms zero production fires post-add). (2) `scripts/hooks/promotion-cycle-trigger.sh:47` `grep -oE 'S[0-9]+'` on basename `promote-rule-S245-L-S240-5-cycle.md` returned TWO matches (S245+S240) → multi-line `LAST_PROMOTE_SESSION` → line 51 `$((..))` syntax error → SESSION_DELTA unbound at line 55.\n**Rule**: (1) When wiring env-vars in Claude Code hook commands, prepend `env` (e.g. `env VAR=val bash \"...\"`) — DO NOT rely on inline POSIX env-prefix syntax which the hook spawn layer mishandles. (2) When parsing session-IDs from `promote-rule-S<N>-*.md` filenames, ALWAYS pipe `grep -oE 'S[0-9]+'` through `| head -1` because filename may contain referenced lesson IDs (`L-S<M>-*`) producing multi-line output that breaks downstream arithmetic.\n**Anti-example** (1): `\"command\": \"CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT=UserPromptSubmit bash \\\"...\\\"\"` — fails at runtime. (2): `LAST_PROMOTE_SESSION=$(basename ... | grep -oE 'S[0-9]+' | tr -d 'S')` — multi-line on `S245-L-S240` filenames.\n**Correct example** (1): `\"command\": \"env CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT=UserPromptSubmit bash \\\"...\\\"\"` — `env` is a real executable that the spawn layer recognizes. (2): `LAST_PROMOTE_SESSION=$(basename ... | grep -oE 'S[0-9]+' | head -1 | tr -d 'S')`.\n**Why**: Both are AP-23 family \"harness ships with verification-time defect because firing-test fixture didn't exercise actual-Windows-spawn-context or actual-filename-edge-case.\" Class instance count: 5+ (M-S189-1, S243 lock-trap, S245 lock-pid, S245 env-prefix, S245 grep-multi-S). AP-23 promotion threshold OVERDUE.\n**How to apply**: Both fixes shipped this session (settings.json + promotion-cycle-trigger.sh edits, smoke-tested EXIT=0). Promote-rule cycle queued for next session to formalize \"env-wrapper required pattern\" + \"head-1-after-grep-on-multi-token-filename pattern\" as bash-hook-lint extensions.\n**Severity**: high. **Auto-detect**: yes — two new lint targets for next bash-hook-lint extension (S246+).","numLines":100,"startLine":1,"totalLines":696}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"899825d9-0bd5-4350-af85-ba8e1c061957","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"ff7c6871-e690-4fdf-bb47-521c4b06efa1","version":"2.1.138","gitBranch":"main"}

Emits JSON decision:block on divergence.\n5\t# L-S66-1 codification. L-S58-1 ERR-trap-aware: uses if/then/fi not [ x ] && Y.\n6\t# L-S11-1 portability: bash + awk + grep + sed only (no python3/jq/yq).\n7\t#\n8\t# Wired: SubagentStop (after subagent-stop-logger.sh, before cost-ledger-recorder.sh)\n9\t# Kill switch: STOCKFORGE_ATTESTATION_DISABLE=1\n10\t# Strict mode: STOCKFORGE_ATTESTATION_STRICT=1 → block on warn-only conditions\n11\t# bash-hook-lint:allow L-S11-1 python (not python3) used as fallback for pytest; guarded by `command -v python` check; WARN-only if unavailable.\n12\t\n13\tset -uo pipefail\n14\ttrap 'exit 0' ERR\n15\t\n16\tPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\n17\tMEMORY_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\n18\tOBS_DIR=\"$MEMORY_DIR/observations\"\n19\tATTEST_LOG=\"$MEMORY_DIR/attestation-log.tsv\"\n20\tATTEST_MARKER_DIR=\"$MEMORY_DIR\"\n21\t\n22\t# Kill switch\n23\tif [ \"${STOCKFORGE_ATTESTATION_DISABLE:-0}\" = \"1\" ]; then\n24\t  exit 0\n25\tfi\n26\t\n27\tPAYLOAD=\"$(cat)\"\n28\tif [ -z \"$PAYLOAD\" ]; then\n29\t  exit 0\n30\tfi\n31\t\n32\t# Parse hook event — grep exits 1 if not found; use || true\n33\tHOOK_EVENT=\"$(printf '%s' \"$PAYLOAD\" | grep -o '\"hook_event_name\":\"[^\"]*\"' 2>/dev/null | head -1 | sed 's/\"hook_event_name\":\"//;s/\"//' || true)\"\n34\tif [ \"$HOOK_EVENT\" != \"SubagentStop\" ]; then\n35\t  exit 0\n36\tfi\n37\t\n38\t# Find latest sandwich-dev observation file (newest mtime)\n39\tif [ ! -d \"$OBS_DIR\" ]; then\n40\t  exit 0\n41\tfi\n42\t\n43\tLATEST_OBS=\"\"\n44\tLATEST_TS=0\n45\tfor f in \"$OBS_DIR\"/sandwich-dev-*.md; do\n46\t  if [ -f \"$f\" ]; then\n47\t    FILE_TS=\"$(stat -c%Y \"$f\" 2>/dev/null || stat -f%m \"$f\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\"\n48\t    if [ \"$FILE_TS\" -gt \"$LATEST_TS\" ]; then\n49\t      LATEST_TS=\"$FILE_TS\"\n50\t      LATEST_OBS=\"$f\"\n51\t    fi\n52\t  fi\n53\tdone\n54\t\n55\tif [ -z \"$LATEST_OBS\" ]; then\n56\t  exit 0\n57\tfi\n58\t\n59\t# Check if already processed\n60\tOBS_BASENAME=\"$(basename \"$LATEST_OBS\" .md)\"\n61\tMARKER_FILE=\"$ATTEST_MARKER_DIR/.attestation-checked-${OBS_BASENAME}\"\n62\tif [ -f \"$MARKER_FILE\" ]; then\n63\t  exit 0\n64\tfi\n65\t\n66\t# TC6: Check for structured frontmatter at file start (line 1 must be ---)\n67\t# Use awk so empty/missing file collapses to 0 cleanly; grep -c || echo 0 emits \"0\\n0\" on no-match.\n68\tHAS_FRONTMATTER=\"$(awk 'NR==1{if($0==\"---\") print 1; else print 0; exit}' \"$LATEST_OBS\" 2>/dev/null || printf '0')\"\n69\tif [ -z \"$HAS_FRONTMATTER\" ]; then HAS_FRONTMATTER=0; fi\n70\tif [ \"$HAS_FRONTMATTER\" -lt 1 ] 2>/dev/null; then\n71\t  printf 'WARN: observation %s has no frontmatter — legacy format. Upgrade to structured schema per L-S66-1.\\n' \"$OBS_BASENAME\" >&2\n72\t  if [ \"${STOCKFORGE_ATTESTATION_STRICT:-0}\" = \"1\" ]; then\n73\t    printf '{\"decision\":\"block\",\"reason\":\"legacy-observation-format\",\"observation_file\":\"%s\"}\\n' \"$OBS_BASENAME\"\n74\t    exit 2\n75\t  fi\n76\t  exit 0\n77\tfi\n78\t\n79\t# Extract frontmatter block (between first two --- lines)\n80\tFRONTMATTER=\"$(awk '/^---/{c++;if(c==2)exit;next}c==1{print}' \"$LATEST_OBS\" 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n81\tif [ -z \"$FRONTMATTER\" ]; then\n82\t  printf 'WARN: observation %s frontmatter empty — legacy format. Upgrade per L-S66-1.\\n' \"$OBS_BASENAME\" >&2\n83\t  if [ \"${STOCKFORGE_ATTESTATION_STRICT:-0}\" = \"1\" ]; then\n84\t    printf '{\"decision\":\"block\",\"reason\":\"empty-frontmatter\",\"observation_file\":\"%s\"}\\n' \"$OBS_BASENAME\"\n85\t    exit 2\n86\t  fi\n87\t  exit 0\n88\tfi\n89\t\n90\t# Extract observation_id using awk (avoids grep exit-1 on no match)\n91\tOBS_ID=\"$(printf '%s' \"$FRONTMATTER\" | awk '/^observation_id:/{sub(/^observation_id:[[:space:]]*/,\"\");print;exit}' || true)\"\n92\tDISPATCH_ID=\"${OBS_ID:-${OBS_BASENAME}}\"\n93\t\n94\t# TC7: check dispatch_id mismatch\n95\tSESSION_ID=\"$(printf '%s' \"$PAYLOAD\" | grep -o '\"session_id\":\"[^\"]*\"' 2>/dev/null | head -1 | sed 's/\"session_id\":\"//;s/\"//' || true)\"\n96\tif [ -n \"${SESSION_ID:-}\" ]; Emits JSON decision:block on divergence.\n# L-S66-1 codification. L-S58-1 ERR-trap-aware: uses if/then/fi not [ x ] && Y.\n# L-S11-1 portability: bash + awk + grep + sed only (no python3/jq/yq).\n#\n# Wired: SubagentStop (after subagent-stop-logger.sh, before cost-ledger-recorder.sh)\n# Kill switch: STOCKFORGE_ATTESTATION_DISABLE=1\n# Strict mode: STOCKFORGE_ATTESTATION_STRICT=1 → block on warn-only conditions\n# bash-hook-lint:allow L-S11-1 python (not python3) used as fallback for pytest; guarded by `command -v python` check; WARN-only if unavailable.\n\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nMEMORY_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nOBS_DIR=\"$MEMORY_DIR/observations\"\nATTEST_LOG=\"$MEMORY_DIR/attestation-log.tsv\"\nATTEST_MARKER_DIR=\"$MEMORY_DIR\"\n\n# Kill switch\nif [ \"${STOCKFORGE_ATTESTATION_DISABLE:-0}\" = \"1\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\nPAYLOAD=\"$(cat)\"\nif [ -z \"$PAYLOAD\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Parse hook event — grep exits 1 if not found; use || true\nHOOK_EVENT=\"$(printf '%s' \"$PAYLOAD\" | grep -o '\"hook_event_name\":\"[^\"]*\"' 2>/dev/null | head -1 | sed 's/\"hook_event_name\":\"//;s/\"//' || true)\"\nif [ \"$HOOK_EVENT\" != \"SubagentStop\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Find latest sandwich-dev observation file (newest mtime)\nif [ ! -d \"$OBS_DIR\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\nLATEST_OBS=\"\"\nLATEST_TS=0\nfor f in \"$OBS_DIR\"/sandwich-dev-*.md; do\n  if [ -f \"$f\" ]; then\n    FILE_TS=\"$(stat -c%Y \"$f\" 2>/dev/null || stat -f%m \"$f\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\"\n    if [ \"$FILE_TS\" -gt \"$LATEST_TS\" ]; then\n      LATEST_TS=\"$FILE_TS\"\n      LATEST_OBS=\"$f\"\n    fi\n  fi\ndone\n\nif [ -z \"$LATEST_OBS\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Check if already processed\nOBS_BASENAME=\"$(basename \"$LATEST_OBS\" .md)\"\nMARKER_FILE=\"$ATTEST_MARKER_DIR/.attestation-checked-${OBS_BASENAME}\"\nif [ -f \"$MARKER_FILE\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# TC6: Check for structured frontmatter at file start (line 1 must be ---)\n# Use awk so empty/missing file collapses to 0 cleanly; grep -c || echo 0 emits \"0\\n0\" on no-match.\nHAS_FRONTMATTER=\"$(awk 'NR==1{if($0==\"---\") print 1; else print 0; exit}' \"$LATEST_OBS\" 2>/dev/null || printf '0')\"\nif [ -z \"$HAS_FRONTMATTER\" ]; then HAS_FRONTMATTER=0; fi\nif [ \"$HAS_FRONTMATTER\" -lt 1 ] 2>/dev/null; then\n  printf 'WARN: observation %s has no frontmatter — legacy format. Upgrade to structured schema per L-S66-1.\\n' \"$OBS_BASENAME\" >&2\n  if [ \"${STOCKFORGE_ATTESTATION_STRICT:-0}\" = \"1\" ]; then\n    printf '{\"decision\":\"block\",\"reason\":\"legacy-observation-format\",\"observation_file\":\"%s\"}\\n' \"$OBS_BASENAME\"\n    exit 2\n  fi\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Extract frontmatter block (between first two --- lines)\nFRONTMATTER=\"$(awk '/^---/{c++;if(c==2)exit;next}c==1{print}' \"$LATEST_OBS\" 2>/dev/null || true)\"\nif [ -z \"$FRONTMATTER\" ]; then\n  printf 'WARN: observation %s frontmatter empty — legacy format. Upgrade per L-S66-1.\\n' \"$OBS_BASENAME\" >&2\n  if [ \"${STOCKFORGE_ATTESTATION_STRICT:-0}\" = \"1\" ]; then\n    printf '{\"decision\":\"block\",\"reason\":\"empty-frontmatter\",\"observation_file\":\"%s\"}\\n' \"$OBS_BASENAME\"\n    exit 2\n  fi\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Extract observation_id using awk (avoids grep exit-1 on no match)\nOBS_ID=\"$(printf '%s' \"$FRONTMATTER\" | awk '/^observation_id:/{sub(/^observation_id:[[:space:]]*/,\"\");print;exit}' || true)\"\nDISPATCH_ID=\"${OBS_ID:-${OBS_BASENAME}}\"\n\n# TC7: check dispatch_id mismatch\nSESSION_ID=\"$(printf '%s' \"$PAYLOAD\" | grep -o '\"session_id\":\"[^\"]*\"' 2>/dev/null | head -1 | sed 's/\"session_id\":\"//;s/\"//' || true)\"\nif [ -n \"${SESSION_ID:-}\" ]; then\n  SIDECAR=\"$MEMORY_DIR/.dispatch-pending-${SESSION_ID}.jsonl\"\n  if [ -f \"$SIDECAR\" ]; then\n    SIDECAR_DID=\"$(tail -1 \"$SIDECAR\" 2>/dev/null | grep -o '\"dispatch_id\":\"[^\"]*\"' 2>/dev/null | head -1 | sed 's/\"dispatch_id\":\"//;s/\"//' || true)\"\n    if [ -n \"${SIDECAR_DID:-}\" ] && [ \"$SIDECAR_DID\" != \"$DISPATCH_ID\" ]; then\n      exit 0\n    fi\n  fi\nfi\n\n# Parse claimed test counts using awk (no grep exit-1 risk)\n# Try bc6_pytest_passed + bc7_pytest_passed first; fall back to tests_passed\nCLAIMED_PASSED=\"$(printf '%s' \"$FRONTMATTER\" | awk '\n  /^bc6_pytest_passed:/ { split($0,a,\":\"); sum+=a[2]+0 }\n  /^bc7_pytest_passed:/ { split($0,a,\":\"); sum+=a[2]+0 }\n  END { print sum+0 }\n' || true)\"\nif [ -z \"${CLAIMED_PASSED:-}\" ]; then\n  CLAIMED_PASSED=0\nfi\n\n# If no bc6/bc7 fields (sum=0), fall back to tests_passed\nif [ \"${CLAIMED_PASSED}\" -eq 0 ] 2>/dev/null; then\n  CLAIMED_PASSED=\"$(printf '%s' \"$FRONTMATTER\" | awk '/^tests_passed:/{split($0,a,\":\");v=a[2]+0;if(v>0)print v;exit}' || true)\"\n  if [ -z \"${CLAIMED_PASSED:-}\" ]; then\n    CLAIMED_PASSED=0\n  fi\nfi\n\nCLAIMED_FAILED=\"$(printf '%s' \"$FRONTMATTER\" | awk '/^tests_failed:/{split($0,a,\":\");print a[2]+0;exit}' || true)\"\nif [ -z \"${CLAIMED_FAILED:-}\" ]; then\n  CLAIMED_FAILED=0\nfi\n\n# Parse file counts\nFILES_CREATED=\"$(printf '%s' \"$FRONTMATTER\" | awk '/^files_created:/{split($0,a,\":\");v=a[2]+0;print v;exit}' || true)\"\nFILES_MODIFIED=\"$(printf '%s' \"$FRONTMATTER\" | awk '/^files_modified:/{split($0,a,\":\");v=a[2]+0;print v;exit}' || true)\"\nif [ -z \"${FILES_CREATED:-}\" ]; then FILES_CREATED=0; fi\nif [ -z \"${FILES_MODIFIED:-}\" ]; then FILES_MODIFIED=0; fi\nCLAIMED_FILE_COUNT=$(( FILES_CREATED + FILES_MODIFIED ))\n\nTS=\"$(date -u +%FT%T.%3NZ 2>/dev/null || date -u +%FT%TZ 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n\n# TC5: check pytest availability\nPYTEST_BIN=\"\"\nif command -v pytest >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n  PYTEST_BIN=\"pytest\"\nelif command -v python >/dev/null 2>&1 && python -m pytest --version >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n  PYTEST_BIN=\"python -m pytest\"\nfi\n\nappend_log_row() {\n  local row_ts=\"$1\" row_did=\"$2\" row_claim=\"$3\" row_empirical=\"$4\" row_div=\"$5\" row_verdict=\"$6\"\n  if [ ! -f \"$ATTEST_LOG\" ]; then\n    mkdir -p \"$(dirname \"$ATTEST_LOG\")\"\n    printf 'ts\\tdispatch_id\\tobservation_passed\\tempirical_passed\\tdivergence\\tverdict\\n' > \"$ATTEST_LOG\"\n  fi\n  printf '%s\\t%s\\t%s\\t%s\\t%s\\t%s\\n' \\\n    \"$row_ts\" \"$row_did\" \"$row_claim\" \"$row_empirical\" \"$row_div\" \"$row_verdict\" >> \"$ATTEST_LOG\"\n}\n\nif [ -z \"$PYTEST_BIN\" ]; then\n  printf 'WARN: pytest not found — skipping empirical attestation check for %s\\n' \"$OBS_BASENAME\" >&2\n  append_log_row \"$TS\" \"$DISPATCH_ID\" \"$CLAIMED_PASSED\" \"SKIP\" \"SKIP\" \"WARN-NO-PYTEST\"\n  touch \"$MARKER_FILE\"\n  if [ \"${STOCKFORGE_ATTESTATION_STRICT:-0}\" = \"1\" ]; then\n    printf '{\"decision\":\"block\",\"reason\":\"pytest-unavailable\",\"observation_file\":\"%s\"}\\n' \"$OBS_BASENAME\"\n    exit 2\n  fi\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Default BC paths for stockforge sandwich-dev (BC-6 + BC-7)\n# BC-6 = Influence (KOL + telegram_adapter); BC-7 = Crowd (sentiment + pump detection).\nBC6_PATHS=\"packages/domain/influence packages/application/influence packages/infrastructure/influence\"\nBC7_PATHS=\"packages/domain/crowd packages/application/crowd packages/infrastructure/crowd\"\n\n# Build pytest paths that actually exist\nPYTEST_PATHS=\"\"\nfor p in $BC6_PATHS $BC7_PATHS; do\n  FULL_P=\"$PROJECT_DIR/$p\"\n  if [ -d \"$FULL_P\" ]; then\n    PYTEST_PATHS=\"$PYTEST_PATHS $FULL_P\"\n  fi\ndone\n\nEMPIRICAL_PASSED=0\nif [ -n \"$PYTEST_PATHS\" ]; then\n  PYTEST_CMD=\"$PYTEST_BIN --tb=no -q\"\n  # Run pytest; allow failure (tests may fail); capture output\n  PYTEST_OUT=\"$(eval \"$PYTEST_CMD $PYTEST_PATHS\" 2>&1 || true)\"\n  # Extract pass count using awk on summary line (e.g. \"172 passed, 0 failed\")\n  EMPIRICAL_PASSED=\"$(printf '%s' \"$PYTEST_OUT\" | awk '/[0-9]+ passed/{match($0,/([0-9]+) passed/,a);if(a[1]+0>0){print a[1]+0;exit}}' || true)\"\n  if [ -z \"${EMPIRICAL_PASSED:-}\" ]; then\n    # Fallback: look for just digits at start of line (pytest -q summary)\n    EMPIRICAL_PASSED=\"$(printf '%s' \"$PYTEST_OUT\" | awk '/^[0-9]+ passed/{print $1+0;exit}' || true)\"\n  fi\n  if [ -z \"${EMPIRICAL_PASSED:-}\" ]; then\n    EMPIRICAL_PASSED=0\n  fi\nfi\n\n# Count Python files in BC paths\nEMPIRICAL_FILE_COUNT=0\nfor p in $BC6_PATHS $BC7_PATHS; do\n  FULL_P=\"$PROJECT_DIR/$p\"\n  if [ -d \"$FULL_P\" ]; then\n    CNT=\"$(find \"$FULL_P\" -name '*.py' -not -path '*/__pycache__/*' 2>/dev/null | wc -l || echo 0)\"\n    EMPIRICAL_FILE_COUNT=$(( EMPIRICAL_FILE_COUNT + CNT ))\n  fi\ndone\n\n# Compute divergences\nTEST_DIVERGENCE=$(( EMPIRICAL_PASSED - CLAIMED_PASSED ))\nif [ \"$TEST_DIVERGENCE\" -lt 0 ]; then\n  TEST_DIVERGENCE=$(( -TEST_DIVERGENCE ))\nfi\n\nFILE_DIVERGENCE=0\nif [ \"$CLAIMED_FILE_COUNT\" -gt 0 ]; then\n  FILE_DIVERGENCE=$(( EMPIRICAL_FILE_COUNT - CLAIMED_FILE_COUNT ))\n  if [ \"$FILE_DIVERGENCE\" -lt 0 ]; then\n    FILE_DIVERGENCE=$(( -FILE_DIVERGENCE ))\n  fi\nfi\n\nVERDICT=\"PASS\"\nBLOCK_REASON=\"\"\n\n# TC2: test count divergence >= 1 → block\nif [ \"$TEST_DIVERGENCE\" -ge 1 ]; then\n  VERDICT=\"BLOCK\"\n  BLOCK_REASON=\"test_count_divergence:${TEST_DIVERGENCE}(claimed=${CLAIMED_PASSED},empirical=${EMPIRICAL_PASSED})\"\nfi\n\n# TC3: file count divergence >= 3 → block\nif [ \"$FILE_DIVERGENCE\" -ge 3 ]; then\n  VERDICT=\"BLOCK\"\n  if [ -n \"$BLOCK_REASON\" ]; then\n    BLOCK_REASON=\"${BLOCK_REASON}|file_count_divergence:${FILE_DIVERGENCE}\"\n  else\n    BLOCK_REASON=\"file_count_divergence:${FILE_DIVERGENCE}(empirical_total=${EMPIRICAL_FILE_COUNT},claimed_delta=${CLAIMED_FILE_COUNT})\"\n  fi\nfi\n\nappend_log_row \"$TS\" \"$DISPATCH_ID\" \"$CLAIMED_PASSED\" \"$EMPIRICAL_PASSED\" \"$TEST_DIVERGENCE\" \"$VERDICT\"\ntouch \"$MARKER_FILE\"\n\nif [ \"$VERDICT\" = \"BLOCK\" ]; fixtures use printf/cat heredoc.\n10\t# 8 test cases per Plan 011 D1 acceptance.\n11\tset -euo pipefail\n12\t\n13\tSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\n14\tHOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../post-dev-dispatch-attestation-check.sh\"\n15\t[ ! -f \"$HOOK\" ] && { echo \"FAIL: hook not found at $HOOK\"; exit 1; }\n16\t\n17\tTEMPDIR=\"$(mktemp -d)\"\n18\ttrap 'rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR\"' EXIT\n19\t\n20\tPROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/proj\"\n21\tOBS_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/observations\"\n22\tATTEST_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/attestation-log.tsv\"\n23\tmkdir -p \"$OBS_DIR\"\n24\tmkdir -p \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\n25\t\n26\tPASS=0\n27\tFAIL=0\n28\t\n29\trun_hook() {\n30\t  local payload=\"$1\"\n31\t  local rc=0\n32\t  HOOK_STDOUT=\"$(CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" <<< \"$payload\" 2>/dev/null)\" || rc=$?\n33\t  HOOK_RC=$rc\n34\t}\n35\t\n36\tclean_obs() {\n37\t  rm -f \"$OBS_DIR\"/sandwich-dev-*.md\n38\t  rm -f \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.attestation-checked-\"*\n39\t  if [ -f \"$ATTEST_LOG\" ]; then\n40\t    # Keep header, remove data rows\n41\t    head -1 \"$ATTEST_LOG\" > \"${ATTEST_LOG}.tmp\" && mv \"${ATTEST_LOG}.tmp\" \"$ATTEST_LOG\" || true\n42\t  fi\n43\t}\n44\t\n45\tmake_obs() {\n46\t  local name=\"$1\" claimed_passed=\"$2\" claimed_failed=\"$3\" files_created=\"$4\" files_modified=\"$5\"\n47\t  cat > \"$OBS_DIR/${name}.md\" <<EOF\n48\t---\n49\tobservation_id: ${name}\n50\tagent: sandwich-dev\n51\tmodel: sonnet\n52\ttests_passed: ${claimed_passed}\n53\ttests_failed: ${claimed_failed}\n54\tfiles_created: ${files_created}\n55\tfiles_modified: ${files_modified}\n56\tmypy_clean: true\n57\tverdict: READY-FOR-MAIN-VERIFY\n58\t---\n59\t\n60\t## Body\n61\t\n62\tTest observation body.\n63\tEOF\n64\t}\n65\t\n66\tSUBAGENT_STOP_PAYLOAD='{\"hook_event_name\":\"SubagentStop\",\"session_id\":\"test-sess\",\"status\":\"DONE\"}'\n67\t\n68\t# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n69\t# TC1 — ground-truth match: claimed=0, empirical=0 → PASS verdict, no block.\n70\t# The hook uses CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=$PROJECT_DIR (temp dir); BC paths don't exist\n71\t# there, so empirical=0. claimed=0 → divergence=0 → PASS.\n72\t# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n73\tclean_obs\n74\tmake_obs \"sandwich-dev-S99-tc1\" 0 0 0 0\n75\trun_hook \"$SUBAGENT_STOP_PAYLOAD\"\n76\t\n77\tif [ \"$HOOK_RC\" -ne 0 ]; then\n78\t  echo \"FAIL TC1: hook exited $HOOK_RC (expected 0 for ground-truth match)\"\n79\t  FAIL=$((FAIL+1))\n80\telif [ -f \"$ATTEST_LOG\" ] && grep -q \"sandwich-dev-S99-tc1\" \"$ATTEST_LOG\" && grep \"sandwich-dev-S99-tc1\" \"$ATTEST_LOG\" | grep -q \"PASS\"; then\n81\t  echo \"PASS TC1: ground-truth match (claimed=0, empirical=0) → PASS verdict, exit 0\"\n82\t  PASS=$((PASS+1))\n83\telse\n84\t  echo \"FAIL TC1: log row missing or incorrect verdict\"\n85\t  cat \"$ATTEST_LOG\" 2>/dev/null || echo \"(no log)\"\n86\t  FAIL=$((FAIL+1))\n87\tfi\n88\t\n89\t# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n90\t# TC2 — +5 PASS divergence → block (empirical=0, claimed=5)\n91\t# Hook will find 0 empirical (no BC paths in temp dir), claimed=5 → divergence=5≥1 → block\n92\t# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n93\tclean_obs\n94\tmake_obs \"sandwich-dev-S99-tc2\" 5 0 1 1\n95\tHOOK_STDOUT=\"\"\n96\tHOOK_RC=0\n97\tHOOK_STDOUT=\"$(CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" <<< \"$SUBAGENT_STOP_PAYLOAD\" 2>/dev/null)\" || HOOK_RC=$?\n98\t\n99\tif [ \"$HOOK_RC\" -eq 2 ]; then\n100\t  # Verify block JSON emitted\n101\t  if printf '%s' \"$HOOK_STDOUT\" | grep -q '\"decision\":\"block\"'; then\n102\t    echo \"PASS TC2: +5 divergence → block + decision:block JSON emitted\"\n103\t    PASS=$((PASS+1))\n104\t  else\n105\t    echo \"FAIL TC2: exit 2 but no block JSON on stdout\"\n106\t    printf 'stdout: %s\\n' \"$HOOK_STDOUT\"\n107\t    FAIL=$((FAIL+1))\n108\t  fi\n109\telif [ \"$HOOK_RC\" -eq 0 ]; then\n110\t  echo \"FAIL TC2: expected exit 2 (block), got 0\"\n111\t  FAIL=$((FAIL+1))\n112\telse\n113\t  echo \"FAIL TC2: unexpected exit code $HOOK_RC\"\n114\t  FAIL=$((FAIL+1))\n115\tfi\n116\t\n117\t# Verify log row has BLOCK verdict\n118\tif [ -f \"$ATTEST_LOG\" ] && grep -q \"sandwich-dev-S99-tc2\" \"$ATTEST_LOG\" && grep \"sandwich-dev-S99-tc2\" \"$ATTEST_LOG\" | grep -q \"BLOCK\"; then\n119\t  echo \"  TC2 log: BLOCK verdict confirmed in attestation-log.tsv\"\n120\telse\n121\t  echo \"  TC2 log: WARNING — BLOCK not in log (attestation-log verification)\"\n122\tfi\n123\t\n124\t# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n125\t# TC3 — file-count divergence → block\n126\t# claimed_passed=0 (no divergence), but files_modified=50 → empirical file count\n127\t# will differ. We need files_modified large enough that empirical(0) diverges by >=3.\n128\t# claimed delta (files_created+files_modified) = 50 > empirical(0) → |50-0| = 50 >=3 → block\n129\t# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n130\tclean_obs\n131\tmake_obs \"sandwich-dev-S99-tc3\" 0 0 0 50\n132\tHOOK_STDOUT=\"\"\n133\tHOOK_RC=0\n134\tHOOK_STDOUT=\"$(CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" <<< \"$SUBAGENT_STOP_PAYLOAD\" 2>/dev/null)\" || HOOK_RC=$?\n135\t\n136\tif [ \"$HOOK_RC\" -eq 2 ] && printf '%s' \"$HOOK_STDOUT\" | grep -q '\"decision\":\"block\"'; then\n137\t  echo \"PASS TC3: file-count divergence ≥3 → block\"\n138\t  PASS=$((PASS+1))\n139\telse\n140\t  echo \"FAIL TC3: expected block on file divergence, got rc=$HOOK_RC stdout='$HOOK_STDOUT'\"\n141\t  FAIL=$((FAIL+1))\n142\tfi\n143\t\n144\t# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n145\t# TC4 — no observation file → exit 0 (no-op)\n146\t# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n147\tclean_obs\n148\t# Don't create any obs file\n149\tHOOK_STDOUT=\"\"\n150\tHOOK_RC=0\n151\tHOOK_STDOUT=\"$(CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" <<< \"$SUBAGENT_STOP_PAYLOAD\" 2>/dev/null)\" || HOOK_RC=$?\n152\t\n153\tif [ \"$HOOK_RC\" -eq 0 ]; then\n154\t  echo \"PASS TC4: no observation file → exit 0 (no-op)\"\n155\t  PASS=$((PASS+1))\n156\telse\n157\t  echo \"FAIL TC4: expected exit 0, got $HOOK_RC\"\n158\t  FAIL=$((FAIL+1))\n159\tfi\n160\t\n161\t# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n162\t# TC5 — pytest missing → WARN + graceful skip (WARN-NO-PYTEST in log)\n163\t# We test by pointing HOOK at a temp project where PATH has no pytest.\n164\t# Simulate by wrapping with PATH override. Also verify WARN emitted to stderr.\n165\t# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n166\tclean_obs\n167\tmake_obs \"sandwich-dev-S99-tc5\" 10 0 1 1\n168\tHOOK_STDERR=\"\"\n169\tHOOK_RC=0\n170\tHOOK_STDERR=\"$(PATH=/usr/bin:/bin CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" <<< \"$SUBAGENT_STOP_PAYLOAD\" 2>&1 >/dev/null)\" || HOOK_RC=$?\n171\t\n172\tif [ \"$HOOK_RC\" -eq 0 ]; then\n173\t  # Check for WARN in stderr\n174\t  if printf '%s' \"$HOOK_STDERR\" | grep -q \"WARN\"; then\n175\t    echo \"PASS TC5: pytest missing → graceful skip + WARN on stderr\"\n176\t    PASS=$((PASS+1))\n177\t  else\n178\t    # If pytest IS available in this environment, skip the test gracefully\n179\t    if command -v pytest >/dev/null 2>&1 || python -m pytest --version >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n180\t      echo \"SKIP TC5: pytest available in environment — cannot simulate missing pytest; PASS treated as skip\"\n181\t      PASS=$((PASS+1))\n182\t    else\n183\t      echo \"FAIL TC5: exit 0 but no WARN on stderr when pytest missing\"\n184\t      FAIL=$((FAIL+1))\n185\t    fi\n186\t  fi\n187\telse\n188\t  echo \"FAIL TC5: expected exit 0 on missing pytest, got $HOOK_RC\"\n189\t  FAIL=$((FAIL+1))\n190\tfi\n191\t\n192\t# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n193\t# TC6 — unstructured legacy observation → upgrade-prompt\n194\t# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n195\tclean_obs\n196\t# Create obs file without frontmatter\n197\tcat > \"$OBS_DIR/sandwich-dev-S99-tc6.md\" <<'EOF'\n198\tSession: S99\n199\t\n200\tAll tests passed. 172 passed, 0 failed.
