fixtures use printf/cat heredoc.\n# 8 test cases per Plan 011 D1 acceptance.\nset -euo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nHOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../post-dev-dispatch-attestation-check.sh\"\n[ ! -f \"$HOOK\" ] && { echo \"FAIL: hook not found at $HOOK\"; exit 1; }\n\nTEMPDIR=\"$(mktemp -d)\"\ntrap 'rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR\"' EXIT\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/proj\"\nOBS_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/observations\"\nATTEST_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/attestation-log.tsv\"\nmkdir -p \"$OBS_DIR\"\nmkdir -p \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\n\nPASS=0\nFAIL=0\n\nrun_hook() {\n  local payload=\"$1\"\n  local rc=0\n  HOOK_STDOUT=\"$(CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" <<< \"$payload\" 2>/dev/null)\" || rc=$?\n  HOOK_RC=$rc\n}\n\nclean_obs() {\n  rm -f \"$OBS_DIR\"/sandwich-dev-*.md\n  rm -f \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.attestation-checked-\"*\n  if [ -f \"$ATTEST_LOG\" ]; then\n    # Keep header, remove data rows\n    head -1 \"$ATTEST_LOG\" > \"${ATTEST_LOG}.tmp\" && mv \"${ATTEST_LOG}.tmp\" \"$ATTEST_LOG\" || true\n  fi\n}\n\nmake_obs() {\n  local name=\"$1\" claimed_passed=\"$2\" claimed_failed=\"$3\" files_created=\"$4\" files_modified=\"$5\"\n  cat > \"$OBS_DIR/${name}.md\" <<EOF\n---\nobservation_id: ${name}\nagent: sandwich-dev\nmodel: sonnet\ntests_passed: ${claimed_passed}\ntests_failed: ${claimed_failed}\nfiles_created: ${files_created}\nfiles_modified: ${files_modified}\nmypy_clean: true\nverdict: READY-FOR-MAIN-VERIFY\n---\n\n## Body\n\nTest observation body.\nEOF\n}\n\nSUBAGENT_STOP_PAYLOAD='{\"hook_event_name\":\"SubagentStop\",\"session_id\":\"test-sess\",\"status\":\"DONE\"}'\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n# TC1 — ground-truth match: claimed=0, empirical=0 → PASS verdict, no block.\n# The hook uses CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=$PROJECT_DIR (temp dir); BC paths don't exist\n# there, so empirical=0. claimed=0 → divergence=0 → PASS.\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\nclean_obs\nmake_obs \"sandwich-dev-S99-tc1\" 0 0 0 0\nrun_hook \"$SUBAGENT_STOP_PAYLOAD\"\n\nif [ \"$HOOK_RC\" -ne 0 ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: hook exited $HOOK_RC (expected 0 for ground-truth match)\"\n  FAIL=$((FAIL+1))\nelif [ -f \"$ATTEST_LOG\" ] && grep -q \"sandwich-dev-S99-tc1\" \"$ATTEST_LOG\" && grep \"sandwich-dev-S99-tc1\" \"$ATTEST_LOG\" | grep -q \"PASS\"; then\n  echo \"PASS TC1: ground-truth match (claimed=0, empirical=0) → PASS verdict, exit 0\"\n  PASS=$((PASS+1))\nelse\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: log row missing or incorrect verdict\"\n  cat \"$ATTEST_LOG\" 2>/dev/null || echo \"(no log)\"\n  FAIL=$((FAIL+1))\nfi\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n# TC2 — +5 PASS divergence → block (empirical=0, claimed=5)\n# Hook will find 0 empirical (no BC paths in temp dir), claimed=5 → divergence=5≥1 → block\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\nclean_obs\nmake_obs \"sandwich-dev-S99-tc2\" 5 0 1 1\nHOOK_STDOUT=\"\"\nHOOK_RC=0\nHOOK_STDOUT=\"$(CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" <<< \"$SUBAGENT_STOP_PAYLOAD\" 2>/dev/null)\" || HOOK_RC=$?\n\nif [ \"$HOOK_RC\" -eq 2 ]; then\n  # Verify block JSON emitted\n  if printf '%s' \"$HOOK_STDOUT\" | grep -q '\"decision\":\"block\"'; then\n    echo \"PASS TC2: +5 divergence → block + decision:block JSON emitted\"\n    PASS=$((PASS+1))\n  else\n    echo \"FAIL TC2: exit 2 but no block JSON on stdout\"\n    printf 'stdout: %s\\n' \"$HOOK_STDOUT\"\n    FAIL=$((FAIL+1))\n  fi\nelif [ \"$HOOK_RC\" -eq 0 ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: expected exit 2 (block), got 0\"\n  FAIL=$((FAIL+1))\nelse\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: unexpected exit code $HOOK_RC\"\n  FAIL=$((FAIL+1))\nfi\n\n# Verify log row has BLOCK verdict\nif [ -f \"$ATTEST_LOG\" ] && grep -q \"sandwich-dev-S99-tc2\" \"$ATTEST_LOG\" && grep \"sandwich-dev-S99-tc2\" \"$ATTEST_LOG\" | grep -q \"BLOCK\"; then\n  echo \"  TC2 log: BLOCK verdict confirmed in attestation-log.tsv\"\nelse\n  echo \"  TC2 log: WARNING — BLOCK not in log (attestation-log verification)\"\nfi\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n# TC3 — file-count divergence → block\n# claimed_passed=0 (no divergence), but files_modified=50 → empirical file count\n# will differ. We need files_modified large enough that empirical(0) diverges by >=3.\n# claimed delta (files_created+files_modified) = 50 > empirical(0) → |50-0| = 50 >=3 → block\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\nclean_obs\nmake_obs \"sandwich-dev-S99-tc3\" 0 0 0 50\nHOOK_STDOUT=\"\"\nHOOK_RC=0\nHOOK_STDOUT=\"$(CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" <<< \"$SUBAGENT_STOP_PAYLOAD\" 2>/dev/null)\" || HOOK_RC=$?\n\nif [ \"$HOOK_RC\" -eq 2 ] && printf '%s' \"$HOOK_STDOUT\" | grep -q '\"decision\":\"block\"'; then\n  echo \"PASS TC3: file-count divergence ≥3 → block\"\n  PASS=$((PASS+1))\nelse\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: expected block on file divergence, got rc=$HOOK_RC stdout='$HOOK_STDOUT'\"\n  FAIL=$((FAIL+1))\nfi\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n# TC4 — no observation file → exit 0 (no-op)\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\nclean_obs\n# Don't create any obs file\nHOOK_STDOUT=\"\"\nHOOK_RC=0\nHOOK_STDOUT=\"$(CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" <<< \"$SUBAGENT_STOP_PAYLOAD\" 2>/dev/null)\" || HOOK_RC=$?\n\nif [ \"$HOOK_RC\" -eq 0 ]; then\n  echo \"PASS TC4: no observation file → exit 0 (no-op)\"\n  PASS=$((PASS+1))\nelse\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: expected exit 0, got $HOOK_RC\"\n  FAIL=$((FAIL+1))\nfi\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n# TC5 — pytest missing → WARN + graceful skip (WARN-NO-PYTEST in log)\n# We test by pointing HOOK at a temp project where PATH has no pytest.\n# Simulate by wrapping with PATH override. Also verify WARN emitted to stderr.\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\nclean_obs\nmake_obs \"sandwich-dev-S99-tc5\" 10 0 1 1\nHOOK_STDERR=\"\"\nHOOK_RC=0\nHOOK_STDERR=\"$(PATH=/usr/bin:/bin CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" <<< \"$SUBAGENT_STOP_PAYLOAD\" 2>&1 >/dev/null)\" || HOOK_RC=$?\n\nif [ \"$HOOK_RC\" -eq 0 ]; then\n  # Check for WARN in stderr\n  if printf '%s' \"$HOOK_STDERR\" | grep -q \"WARN\"; then\n    echo \"PASS TC5: pytest missing → graceful skip + WARN on stderr\"\n    PASS=$((PASS+1))\n  else\n    # If pytest IS available in this environment, skip the test gracefully\n    if command -v pytest >/dev/null 2>&1 || python -m pytest --version >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n      echo \"SKIP TC5: pytest available in environment — cannot simulate missing pytest; PASS treated as skip\"\n      PASS=$((PASS+1))\n    else\n      echo \"FAIL TC5: exit 0 but no WARN on stderr when pytest missing\"\n      FAIL=$((FAIL+1))\n    fi\n  fi\nelse\n  echo \"FAIL TC5: expected exit 0 on missing pytest, got $HOOK_RC\"\n  FAIL=$((FAIL+1))\nfi\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n# TC6 — unstructured legacy observation → upgrade-prompt\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\nclean_obs\n# Create obs file without frontmatter\ncat > \"$OBS_DIR/sandwich-dev-S99-tc6.md\" <<'EOF'\nSession: S99\n\nAll tests passed. 172 passed, 0 failed. Looks good.\nEOF\n\nHOOK_STDOUT=\"\"\nHOOK_STDERR=\"\"\nHOOK_RC=0\nHOOK_STDOUT=\"$(CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" <<< \"$SUBAGENT_STOP_PAYLOAD\" 2>/tmp/tc6_stderr.txt)\" || HOOK_RC=$?\nHOOK_STDERR=\"$(cat /tmp/tc6_stderr.txt 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n\nif [ \"$HOOK_RC\" -eq 0 ]; then\n  if printf '%s' \"$HOOK_STDERR\" | grep -q \"WARN\"; then\n    echo \"PASS TC6: legacy format → upgrade-prompt WARN + exit 0\"\n    PASS=$((PASS+1))\n  else\n    echo \"FAIL TC6: exit 0 but no WARN upgrade-prompt for legacy format\"\n    printf 'stderr: %s\\n' \"$HOOK_STDERR\"\n    FAIL=$((FAIL+1))\n  fi\nelif [ \"$HOOK_RC\" -eq 2 ]; then\n  # strict mode would block; in non-strict, check that we still got WARN\n  echo \"PASS TC6: legacy format → blocked (or warned+exit0)\"\n  PASS=$((PASS+1))\nelse\n  echo \"FAIL TC6: unexpected exit $HOOK_RC\"\n  FAIL=$((FAIL+1))\nfi\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n# TC7 — dispatch_id mismatch → exit 0 (skip, different dispatch)\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\nclean_obs\n# Create sidecar with different dispatch_id\nmkdir -p \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nprintf '{\"dispatch_id\":\"different-dispatch-xyz\",\"agent_type\":\"sandwich-dev\",\"state\":\"pending\"}\\n' \\\n  > \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.dispatch-pending-test-sess.jsonl\"\n# Create obs with different observation_id\ncat > \"$OBS_DIR/sandwich-dev-S99-tc7.md\" <<'EOF'\n---\nobservation_id: sandwich-dev-S99-tc7-completely-different\nagent: sandwich-dev\ntests_passed: 172\ntests_failed: 0\nfiles_created: 5\nfiles_modified: 3\nmypy_clean: true\nverdict: READY-FOR-MAIN-VERIFY\n---\n\nBody text.\nEOF\n\nHOOK_RC=0\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" <<< \"$SUBAGENT_STOP_PAYLOAD\" >/dev/null 2>&1 || HOOK_RC=$?\nrm -f \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.dispatch-pending-test-sess.jsonl\"\n\nif [ \"$HOOK_RC\" -eq 0 ]; {"parentUuid":"26695063-3383-4c66-8e53-acbb762768cd","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_01L2UX9CVzZYjYizoAuBU1uj","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_014cGmCkyTaU9oj84rbuqczG","name":"Edit","input":{"replace_all":false,"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\post-dev-dispatch-attestation-check.sh","old_string":"# Parse claimed test counts using awk (no grep exit-1 risk)\n# Try bc6_pytest_passed + bc7_pytest_passed first; fall back to tests_passed\nCLAIMED_PASSED=\"$(printf '%s' \"$FRONTMATTER\" | awk '\n  /^bc6_pytest_passed:/ { split($0,a,\":\"); sum+=a[2]+0 }\n  /^bc7_pytest_passed:/ { split($0,a,\":\"); sum+=a[2]+0 }\n  END { print sum+0 }\n' || true)\"\nif [ -z \"${CLAIMED_PASSED:-}\" ]; then\n  CLAIMED_PASSED=0\nfi\n\n# If no bc6/bc7 fields (sum=0), fall back to tests_passed\nif [ \"${CLAIMED_PASSED}\" -eq 0 ] 2>/dev/null; then\n  CLAIMED_PASSED=\"$(printf '%s' \"$FRONTMATTER\" | awk '/^tests_passed:/{split($0,a,\":\");v=a[2]+0;if(v>0)print v;exit}' || true)\"\n  if [ -z \"${CLAIMED_PASSED:-}\" ]; then\n    CLAIMED_PASSED=0\n  fi\nfi","new_string":"# L-S322-2 scope-filter (SHIPPED-IN-S326): out-of-scope obs (harness/lint/hook dev\n# work declaring NO BC pytest coverage) MUST NOT be measured against ambient BC\n# pytest count. Historical FPs at attestation-log rows S237-A2-promote / S320-\n# plan015-S319b-batch-L / S322b-plan016-batch-e-lint-zero — all 0/322/322/BLOCK.\n# Detection: ANY of bc6_pytest_passed / bc7_pytest_passed / tests_passed PRESENT\n# in frontmatter (presence-only — value 0 is a legitimate claim of \"0 passing\").\nHAS_BC6_FIELD=\"$(printf '%s' \"$FRONTMATTER\" | awk '/^bc6_pytest_passed:/{print 1;exit}' || true)\"\nHAS_BC7_FIELD=\"$(printf '%s' \"$FRONTMATTER\" | awk '/^bc7_pytest_passed:/{print 1;exit}' || true)\"\nHAS_TESTS_FIELD=\"$(printf '%s' \"$FRONTMATTER\" | awk '/^tests_passed:/{print 1;exit}' || true)\"\n\nif [ -z \"${HAS_BC6_FIELD:-}\" ] && [ -z \"${HAS_BC7_FIELD:-}\" ] && [ -z \"${HAS_TESTS_FIELD:-}\" ]; then\n  TS_SCOPE=\"$(date -u +%FT%T.%3NZ 2>/dev/null || date -u +%FT%TZ 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n  if [ ! -f \"$ATTEST_LOG\" ]; then\n    mkdir -p \"$(dirname \"$ATTEST_LOG\")\"\n    printf 'ts\\tdispatch_id\\tobservation_passed\\tempirical_passed\\tdivergence\\tverdict\\n' > \"$ATTEST_LOG\"\n  fi\n  printf '%s\\t%s\\t%s\\t%s\\t%s\\t%s\\n' \\\n    \"$TS_SCOPE\" \"$DISPATCH_ID\" \"SKIP-OUT-OF-SCOPE\" \"SKIP\" \"SKIP\" \"SKIP-OUT-OF-SCOPE\" >> \"$ATTEST_LOG\"\n  touch \"$MARKER_FILE\"\n  printf 'INFO: observation %s declares no BC pytest scope (no bc6_pytest_passed/bc7_pytest_passed/tests_passed) — skipping attestation (L-S322-2 scope-filter)\\n' \"$OBS_BASENAME\" >&2\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Parse claimed test counts using awk (no grep exit-1 risk)\n# Try bc6_pytest_passed + bc7_pytest_passed first; fall back to tests_passed\nCLAIMED_PASSED=\"$(printf '%s' \"$FRONTMATTER\" | awk '\n  /^bc6_pytest_passed:/ { split($0,a,\":\"); sum+=a[2]+0 }\n  /^bc7_pytest_passed:/ { split($0,a,\":\"); sum+=a[2]+0 }\n  END { print sum+0 }\n' || true)\"\nif [ -z \"${CLAIMED_PASSED:-}\" ]; then\n  CLAIMED_PASSED=0\nfi\n\n# If no bc6/bc7 fields (sum=0), fall back to tests_passed\nif [ \"${CLAIMED_PASSED}\" -eq 0 ] 2>/dev/null; then\n  CLAIMED_PASSED=\"$(printf '%s' \"$FRONTMATTER\" | awk '/^tests_passed:/{split($0,a,\":\");v=a[2]+0;if(v>0)print v;exit}' || true)\"\n  if [ -z \"${CLAIMED_PASSED:-}\" ]; then\n    CLAIMED_PASSED=0\n  fi\nfi"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2591,"cache_read_input_tokens":131230,"output_tokens":5030,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2591,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[{"input_tokens":1,"output_tokens":5030,"cache_read_input_tokens":131230,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2591,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2591},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011Cb3w8mJVf1TCHBbHWngck","type":"assistant","uuid":"0592b648-cbe4-4771-9c88-a0349173dcb1","timestamp":"2026-05-15T07:08:38.094Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"ff7c6871-e690-4fdf-bb47-521c4b06efa1","version":"2.1.138","gitBranch":"main"}

Historical FPs at attestation-log rows S237-A2-promote / S320-\n# plan015-S319b-batch-L / S322b-plan016-batch-e-lint-zero — all 0/322/322/BLOCK.\n# Detection: ANY of bc6_pytest_passed / bc7_pytest_passed / tests_passed PRESENT\n# in frontmatter (presence-only — value 0 is a legitimate claim of \"0 passing\").\nHAS_BC6_FIELD=\"$(printf '%s' \"$FRONTMATTER\" | awk '/^bc6_pytest_passed:/{print 1;exit}' || true)\"\nHAS_BC7_FIELD=\"$(printf '%s' \"$FRONTMATTER\" | awk '/^bc7_pytest_passed:/{print 1;exit}' || true)\"\nHAS_TESTS_FIELD=\"$(printf '%s' \"$FRONTMATTER\" | awk '/^tests_passed:/{print 1;exit}' || true)\"\n\nif [ -z \"${HAS_BC6_FIELD:-}\" ] && [ -z \"${HAS_BC7_FIELD:-}\" ] && [ -z \"${HAS_TESTS_FIELD:-}\" ]; then\n  TS_SCOPE=\"$(date -u +%FT%T.%3NZ 2>/dev/null || date -u +%FT%TZ 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n  if [ ! -f \"$ATTEST_LOG\" ]; then\n    mkdir -p \"$(dirname \"$ATTEST_LOG\")\"\n    printf 'ts\\tdispatch_id\\tobservation_passed\\tempirical_passed\\tdivergence\\tverdict\\n' > \"$ATTEST_LOG\"\n  fi\n  printf '%s\\t%s\\t%s\\t%s\\t%s\\t%s\\n' \\\n    \"$TS_SCOPE\" \"$DISPATCH_ID\" \"SKIP-OUT-OF-SCOPE\" \"SKIP\" \"SKIP\" \"SKIP-OUT-OF-SCOPE\" >> \"$ATTEST_LOG\"\n  touch \"$MARKER_FILE\"\n  printf 'INFO: observation %s declares no BC pytest scope (no bc6_pytest_passed/bc7_pytest_passed/tests_passed) — skipping attestation (L-S322-2 scope-filter)\\n' \"$OBS_BASENAME\" >&2\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Parse claimed test counts using awk (no grep exit-1 risk)\n# Try bc6_pytest_passed + bc7_pytest_passed first; fall back to tests_passed\nCLAIMED_PASSED=\"$(printf '%s' \"$FRONTMATTER\" | awk '\n  /^bc6_pytest_passed:/ { split($0,a,\":\"); sum+=a[2]+0 }\n  /^bc7_pytest_passed:/ { split($0,a,\":\"); sum+=a[2]+0 }\n  END { print sum+0 }\n' || true)\"\nif [ -z \"${CLAIMED_PASSED:-}\" ]; then\n  CLAIMED_PASSED=0\nfi\n\n# If no bc6/bc7 fields (sum=0), fall back to tests_passed\nif [ \"${CLAIMED_PASSED}\" -eq 0 ] 2>/dev/null; then\n  CLAIMED_PASSED=\"$(printf '%s' \"$FRONTMATTER\" | awk '/^tests_passed:/{split($0,a,\":\");v=a[2]+0;if(v>0)print v;exit}' || true)\"\n  if [ -z \"${CLAIMED_PASSED:-}\" ]; then\n    CLAIMED_PASSED=0\n  fi\nfi","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# post-dev-dispatch-attestation-check.sh — SubagentStop guard for sandwich-dev observations.\n# Reads latest sandwich-dev observation, parses claimed test counts, empirically re-runs\n# pytest on BC paths found in observation, diffs. Emits JSON decision:block on divergence.\n# L-S66-1 codification. L-S58-1 ERR-trap-aware: uses if/then/fi not [ x ] && Y.\n# L-S11-1 portability: bash + awk + grep + sed only (no python3/jq/yq).\n#\n# Wired: SubagentStop (after subagent-stop-logger.sh, before cost-ledger-recorder.sh)\n# Kill switch: STOCKFORGE_ATTESTATION_DISABLE=1\n# Strict mode: STOCKFORGE_ATTESTATION_STRICT=1 → block on warn-only conditions\n# bash-hook-lint:allow L-S11-1 python (not python3) used as fallback for pytest; guarded by `command -v python` check; WARN-only if unavailable.\n\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nMEMORY_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nOBS_DIR=\"$MEMORY_DIR/observations\"\nATTEST_LOG=\"$MEMORY_DIR/attestation-log.tsv\"\nATTEST_MARKER_DIR=\"$MEMORY_DIR\"\n\n# Kill switch\nif [ \"${STOCKFORGE_ATTESTATION_DISABLE:-0}\" = \"1\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\nPAYLOAD=\"$(cat)\"\nif [ -z \"$PAYLOAD\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Parse hook event — grep exits 1 if not found; use || true\nHOOK_EVENT=\"$(printf '%s' \"$PAYLOAD\" | grep -o '\"hook_event_name\":\"[^\"]*\"' 2>/dev/null | head -1 | sed 's/\"hook_event_name\":\"//;s/\"//' || true)\"\nif [ \"$HOOK_EVENT\" != \"SubagentStop\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Find latest sandwich-dev observation file (newest mtime)\nif [ ! -d \"$OBS_DIR\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\nLATEST_OBS=\"\"\nLATEST_TS=0\nfor f in \"$OBS_DIR\"/sandwich-dev-*.md; do\n  if [ -f \"$f\" ]; then\n    FILE_TS=\"$(stat -c%Y \"$f\" 2>/dev/null || stat -f%m \"$f\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\"\n    if [ \"$FILE_TS\" -gt \"$LATEST_TS\" ]; then\n      LATEST_TS=\"$FILE_TS\"\n      LATEST_OBS=\"$f\"\n    fi\n  fi\ndone\n\nif [ -z \"$LATEST_OBS\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Check if already processed\nOBS_BASENAME=\"$(basename \"$LATEST_OBS\" .md)\"\nMARKER_FILE=\"$ATTEST_MARKER_DIR/.attestation-checked-${OBS_BASENAME}\"\nif [ -f \"$MARKER_FILE\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# TC6: Check for structured frontmatter at file start (line 1 must be ---)\n# Use awk so empty/missing file collapses to 0 cleanly; grep -c || echo 0 emits \"0\\n0\" on no-match.\nHAS_FRONTMATTER=\"$(awk 'NR==1{if($0==\"---\") print 1; else print 0; exit}' \"$LATEST_OBS\" 2>/dev/null || printf '0')\"\nif [ -z \"$HAS_FRONTMATTER\" ]; then HAS_FRONTMATTER=0; fi\nif [ \"$HAS_FRONTMATTER\" -lt 1 ] 2>/dev/null; then\n  printf 'WARN: observation %s has no frontmatter — legacy format. Upgrade to structured schema per L-S66-1.\\n' \"$OBS_BASENAME\" >&2\n  if [ \"${STOCKFORGE_ATTESTATION_STRICT:-0}\" = \"1\" ]; then\n    printf '{\"decision\":\"block\",\"reason\":\"legacy-observation-format\",\"observation_file\":\"%s\"}\\n' \"$OBS_BASENAME\"\n    exit 2\n  fi\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Extract frontmatter block (between first two --- lines)\nFRONTMATTER=\"$(awk '/^---/{c++;if(c==2)exit;next}c==1{print}' \"$LATEST_OBS\" 2>/dev/null || true)\"\nif [ -z \"$FRONTMATTER\" ]; then\n  printf 'WARN: observation %s frontmatter empty — legacy format. Upgrade per L-S66-1.\\n' \"$OBS_BASENAME\" >&2\n  if [ \"${STOCKFORGE_ATTESTATION_STRICT:-0}\" = \"1\" ]; then\n    printf '{\"decision\":\"block\",\"reason\":\"empty-frontmatter\",\"observation_file\":\"%s\"}\\n' \"$OBS_BASENAME\"\n    exit 2\n  fi\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Extract observation_id using awk (avoids grep exit-1 on no match)\nOBS_ID=\"$(printf '%s' \"$FRONTMATTER\" | awk '/^observation_id:/{sub(/^observation_id:[[:space:]]*/,\"\");print;exit}' || true)\"\nDISPATCH_ID=\"${OBS_ID:-${OBS_BASENAME}}\"\n\n# TC7: check dispatch_id mismatch\nSESSION_ID=\"$(printf '%s' \"$PAYLOAD\" | grep -o '\"session_id\":\"[^\"]*\"' 2>/dev/null | head -1 | sed 's/\"session_id\":\"//;s/\"//' || true)\"\nif [ -n \"${SESSION_ID:-}\" ]; then\n  SIDECAR=\"$MEMORY_DIR/.dispatch-pending-${SESSION_ID}.jsonl\"\n  if [ -f \"$SIDECAR\" ]; then\n    SIDECAR_DID=\"$(tail -1 \"$SIDECAR\" 2>/dev/null | grep -o '\"dispatch_id\":\"[^\"]*\"' 2>/dev/null | head -1 | sed 's/\"dispatch_id\":\"//;s/\"//' || true)\"\n    if [ -n \"${SIDECAR_DID:-}\" ] && [ \"$SIDECAR_DID\" != \"$DISPATCH_ID\" ]; then\n      exit 0\n    fi\n  fi\nfi\n\n# Parse claimed test counts using awk (no grep exit-1 risk)\n# Try bc6_pytest_passed + bc7_pytest_passed first; fall back to tests_passed\nCLAIMED_PASSED=\"$(printf '%s' \"$FRONTMATTER\" | awk '\n  /^bc6_pytest_passed:/ { split($0,a,\":\"); sum+=a[2]+0 }\n  /^bc7_pytest_passed:/ { split($0,a,\":\"); sum+=a[2]+0 }\n  END { print sum+0 }\n' || true)\"\nif [ -z \"${CLAIMED_PASSED:-}\" ]; then\n  CLAIMED_PASSED=0\nfi\n\n# If no bc6/bc7 fields (sum=0), fall back to tests_passed\nif [ \"${CLAIMED_PASSED}\" -eq 0 ] 2>/dev/null; then\n  CLAIMED_PASSED=\"$(printf '%s' \"$FRONTMATTER\" | awk '/^tests_passed:/{split($0,a,\":\");v=a[2]+0;if(v>0)print v;exit}' || true)\"\n  if [ -z \"${CLAIMED_PASSED:-}\" ]; then\n    CLAIMED_PASSED=0\n  fi\nfi\n\nCLAIMED_FAILED=\"$(printf '%s' \"$FRONTMATTER\" | awk '/^tests_failed:/{split($0,a,\":\");print a[2]+0;exit}' || true)\"\nif [ -z \"${CLAIMED_FAILED:-}\" ]; then\n  CLAIMED_FAILED=0\nfi\n\n# Parse file counts\nFILES_CREATED=\"$(printf '%s' \"$FRONTMATTER\" | awk '/^files_created:/{split($0,a,\":\");v=a[2]+0;print v;exit}' || true)\"\nFILES_MODIFIED=\"$(printf '%s' \"$FRONTMATTER\" | awk '/^files_modified:/{split($0,a,\":\");v=a[2]+0;print v;exit}' || true)\"\nif [ -z \"${FILES_CREATED:-}\" ]; then FILES_CREATED=0; fi\nif [ -z \"${FILES_MODIFIED:-}\" ]; then FILES_MODIFIED=0; fi\nCLAIMED_FILE_COUNT=$(( FILES_CREATED + FILES_MODIFIED ))\n\nTS=\"$(date -u +%FT%T.%3NZ 2>/dev/null || date -u +%FT%TZ 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n\n# TC5: check pytest availability\nPYTEST_BIN=\"\"\nif command -v pytest >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n  PYTEST_BIN=\"pytest\"\nelif command -v python >/dev/null 2>&1 && python -m pytest --version >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n  PYTEST_BIN=\"python -m pytest\"\nfi\n\nappend_log_row() {\n  local row_ts=\"$1\" row_did=\"$2\" row_claim=\"$3\" row_empirical=\"$4\" row_div=\"$5\" row_verdict=\"$6\"\n  if [ ! -f \"$ATTEST_LOG\" ]; then\n    mkdir -p \"$(dirname \"$ATTEST_LOG\")\"\n    printf 'ts\\tdispatch_id\\tobservation_passed\\tempirical_passed\\tdivergence\\tverdict\\n' > \"$ATTEST_LOG\"\n  fi\n  printf '%s\\t%s\\t%s\\t%s\\t%s\\t%s\\n' \\\n    \"$row_ts\" \"$row_did\" \"$row_claim\" \"$row_empirical\" \"$row_div\" \"$row_verdict\" >> \"$ATTEST_LOG\"\n}\n\nif [ -z \"$PYTEST_BIN\" ]; then\n  printf 'WARN: pytest not found — skipping empirical attestation check for %s\\n' \"$OBS_BASENAME\" >&2\n  append_log_row \"$TS\" \"$DISPATCH_ID\" \"$CLAIMED_PASSED\" \"SKIP\" \"SKIP\" \"WARN-NO-PYTEST\"\n  touch \"$MARKER_FILE\"\n  if [ \"${STOCKFORGE_ATTESTATION_STRICT:-0}\" = \"1\" ]; then\n    printf '{\"decision\":\"block\",\"reason\":\"pytest-unavailable\",\"observation_file\":\"%s\"}\\n' \"$OBS_BASENAME\"\n    exit 2\n  fi\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Default BC paths for stockforge sandwich-dev (BC-6 + BC-7)\n# BC-6 = Influence (KOL + telegram_adapter); BC-7 = Crowd (sentiment + pump detection).\nBC6_PATHS=\"packages/domain/influence packages/application/influence packages/infrastructure/influence\"\nBC7_PATHS=\"packages/domain/crowd packages/application/crowd packages/infrastructure/crowd\"\n\n# Build pytest paths that actually exist\nPYTEST_PATHS=\"\"\nfor p in $BC6_PATHS $BC7_PATHS; do\n  FULL_P=\"$PROJECT_DIR/$p\"\n  if [ -d \"$FULL_P\" ]; then\n    PYTEST_PATHS=\"$PYTEST_PATHS $FULL_P\"\n  fi\ndone\n\nEMPIRICAL_PASSED=0\nif [ -n \"$PYTEST_PATHS\" ]; then\n  PYTEST_CMD=\"$PYTEST_BIN --tb=no -q\"\n  # Run pytest; allow failure (tests may fail); capture output\n  PYTEST_OUT=\"$(eval \"$PYTEST_CMD $PYTEST_PATHS\" 2>&1 || true)\"\n  # Extract pass count using awk on summary line (e.g. \"172 passed, 0 failed\")\n  EMPIRICAL_PASSED=\"$(printf '%s' \"$PYTEST_OUT\" | awk '/[0-9]+ passed/{match($0,/([0-9]+) passed/,a);if(a[1]+0>0){print a[1]+0;exit}}' || true)\"\n  if [ -z \"${EMPIRICAL_PASSED:-}\" ]; then\n    # Fallback: look for just digits at start of line (pytest -q summary)\n    EMPIRICAL_PASSED=\"$(printf '%s' \"$PYTEST_OUT\" | awk '/^[0-9]+ passed/{print $1+0;exit}' || true)\"\n  fi\n  if [ -z \"${EMPIRICAL_PASSED:-}\" ]; then\n    EMPIRICAL_PASSED=0\n  fi\nfi\n\n# Count Python files in BC paths\nEMPIRICAL_FILE_COUNT=0\nfor p in $BC6_PATHS $BC7_PATHS; do\n  FULL_P=\"$PROJECT_DIR/$p\"\n  if [ -d \"$FULL_P\" ]; then\n    CNT=\"$(find \"$FULL_P\" -name '*.py' -not -path '*/__pycache__/*' 2>/dev/null | wc -l || echo 0)\"\n    EMPIRICAL_FILE_COUNT=$(( EMPIRICAL_FILE_COUNT + CNT ))\n  fi\ndone\n\n# Compute divergences\nTEST_DIVERGENCE=$(( EMPIRICAL_PASSED - CLAIMED_PASSED ))\nif [ \"$TEST_DIVERGENCE\" -lt 0 ]; then\n  TEST_DIVERGENCE=$(( -TEST_DIVERGENCE ))\nfi\n\nFILE_DIVERGENCE=0\nif [ \"$CLAIMED_FILE_COUNT\" -gt 0 ]; then\n  FILE_DIVERGENCE=$(( EMPIRICAL_FILE_COUNT - CLAIMED_FILE_COUNT ))\n  if [ \"$FILE_DIVERGENCE\" -lt 0 ]; then\n    FILE_DIVERGENCE=$(( -FILE_DIVERGENCE ))\n  fi\nfi\n\nVERDICT=\"PASS\"\nBLOCK_REASON=\"\"\n\n# TC2: test count divergence >= 1 → block\nif [ \"$TEST_DIVERGENCE\" -ge 1 ]; then\n  VERDICT=\"BLOCK\"\n  BLOCK_REASON=\"test_count_divergence:${TEST_DIVERGENCE}(claimed=${CLAIMED_PASSED},empirical=${EMPIRICAL_PASSED})\"\nfi\n\n# TC3: file count divergence >= 3 → block\nif [ \"$FILE_DIVERGENCE\" -ge 3 ]; then\n  VERDICT=\"BLOCK\"\n  if [ -n \"$BLOCK_REASON\" ]; then\n    BLOCK_REASON=\"${BLOCK_REASON}|file_count_divergence:${FILE_DIVERGENCE}\"\n  else\n    BLOCK_REASON=\"file_count_divergence:${FILE_DIVERGENCE}(empirical_total=${EMPIRICAL_FILE_COUNT},claimed_delta=${CLAIMED_FILE_COUNT})\"\n  fi\nfi\n\nappend_log_row \"$TS\" \"$DISPATCH_ID\" \"$CLAIMED_PASSED\" \"$EMPIRICAL_PASSED\" \"$TEST_DIVERGENCE\" \"$VERDICT\"\ntouch \"$MARKER_FILE\"\n\nif [ \"$VERDICT\" = \"BLOCK\" ]; then\n  printf '{\"decision\":\"block\",\"reason\":\"%s\",\"observation_file\":\"%s\",\"claimed_passed\":%s,\"empirical_passed\":%s,\"test_divergence\":%s,\"file_divergence\":%s}\\n' \\\n    \"$BLOCK_REASON\" \"$OBS_BASENAME\" \"$CLAIMED_PASSED\" \"$EMPIRICAL_PASSED\" \"$TEST_DIVERGENCE\" \"$FILE_DIVERGENCE\"\n  exit 2\nfi\n\nexit 0\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":103,"oldLines":6,"newStart":103,"newLines":29,"lines":["   fi"," fi"," ","+# L-S322-2 scope-filter (SHIPPED-IN-S326): out-of-scope obs (harness/lint/hook dev","+# work declaring NO BC pytest coverage) MUST NOT be measured against ambient BC","+# pytest count. Each *-fire-test.sh exits 0 on\n# pass, non-zero on fail. This script iterates all of them with a per-test timeout,\n# tallies results, and returns 0 only if ALL pass.\n#\n# Authored S121 (2026-05-06) to close S120 close-note \"69/69 PASS\" unverifiable claim\n# (no orchestrator existed). Each fire-test self-isolates via TEMPDIR + traps; this\n# runner does not stage fixtures itself.\n#\n# Wires: NOT auto-wired to Stop hook (would slow every turn). Run manually post-hook-edit\n# or via `bash scripts/hooks/firing-tests/run-all.sh` to verify suite state.\n#\n# Phase 0 portability: bash + POSIX only (L-S11-1). No -e (need rc capture per test).\nset -uo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nPER_TEST_TIMEOUT=\"${FIRING_TEST_TIMEOUT:-30}\"\n\n# Detect timeout(1) availability (POSIX-non-portable; GNU coreutils ubiquitous on Linux/git-bash).\nif command -v timeout >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n  USE_TIMEOUT=1\nelse\n  USE_TIMEOUT=0\n  echo \"WARN: timeout(1) not available; running without per-test timeout (slow tests may stall suite)\"\nfi\n\nPASS=0\nFAIL=0\nFAILED_TESTS=\"\"\nTOTAL=0\nSTART_TS=\"$(date +%s 2>/dev/null || echo unknown)\"\n\nfor test in \"$SCRIPT_DIR\"/*-fire-test.sh; do\n  [ ! -f \"$test\" ] && continue\n  bn=\"$(basename \"$test\")\"\n  TOTAL=$((TOTAL + 1))\n\n  if [ \"$USE_TIMEOUT\" = 1 ]; then\n    timeout \"$PER_TEST_TIMEOUT\" bash \"$test\" >/dev/null 2>&1\n  else\n    bash \"$test\" >/dev/null 2>&1\n  fi\n  rc=$?\n\n  if [ \"$rc\" = 0 ]; then\n    PASS=$((PASS + 1))\n  else","numLines":50,"startLine":1,"totalLines":75}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"b2fb46d2-a746-447a-bf65-7ad3fbe6c5b4","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"ff7c6871-e690-4fdf-bb47-521c4b06efa1","version":"2.1.138","gitBranch":"main"}

A prior-session background Agent dispatch CAN survive `/clear` and still be running; the harness sends a task-notification on completion. `dispatch-ledger state:pending` + no observation file is AMBIGUOUS (could be in-flight), NOT proof the agent is dead. Do NOT run verification against a dispatch's partial working-tree footprint until completion is confirmed (task-notification received OR obs file with a complete self-report) — acting on mid-flight state produces inconsistent snapshots and stale downstream briefs. Surfaced S321 (M-S321-1): main treated still-running `aa060792c837dadb7` as lost, verified its mid-flight tree, dispatched a re-verifier with a stale brief; caught on the dev's completion notification, recovered clean. Sister to L-S68-1 + M-S257-1 (orphaned-subagent-survives-/clear). | ACTIVE | medium | inline-S321 |"," | L-S320-1 | Diff-context-comment-contradiction = mandatory-trace red flag. When a diff's context lines include a comment explaining WHY existing code is written a certain way, and the change CONTRADICTS that comment, do NOT pass it as \"genuine\" without resolving the contradiction. Surfaced S320: S319a `daily-backup.sh` `grep -c`→`grep -q` reintroduced a documented SIGPIPE/pipefail false-negative; the warning comment (`:83-86`) was VISIBLE in the same `git diff` hunk; main-session spot-check missed it, fresh-context verifier (AP-1) caught it via empirical reproduction. Reinforces: main-session spot-checks are a sanity gate, NOT a substitute for the fresh-context verifier. Promotion candidate: bash-hook-lint check correlating changed lines vs adjacent comment keywords (`not`, `avoid`, `do NOT`, `instead of`). | ACTIVE | medium | inline-S320 |"," | L-S322-1 | Notification-RESOLVED: severity-classifier.sh Layer 5 had `status:` short-circuit but NOT `level:` short-circuit — author's in-place `level: RESOLVED` edit (M-S322-1) fell through to body-grep and re-tripped on CRITICAL/WARN keywords in body content. SHIPPED-IN-S325: parallel `level:` skip block added at scripts/hooks/severity-classifier.sh:182-186 (after status check, before level→severity case); accepts RESOLVED\\|ANSWERED\\|CLOSED (incl. lowercase). Companion firing-test: TC9 (out-of-scope obs → SKIP-OUT-OF-SCOPE) + TC9b regression-guard (tests_passed:0 is legitimate BC claim, NOT skipped). 10/10 TCs PASS; bash-hook-lint clean; full firing-test suite 103/103 PASS. | SHIPPED-IN-S326 | low | inline-S326 |"," | L-S269-1 | HH-2-USERPROMPT-NOT-FIRING false-positive at SessionStart event — chronic alarm fatigue. `harness-health-self-scan.sh` HH-2 check \"is there ≥1 UserPromptSubmit in HOOK_LOG within last 10 min\" runs on SessionStart event, but at SessionStart the current turn's UP hook hasn't fired yet (UP fires AFTER SessionStart on fresh `/clear`). Sessions where prior UP→UP gap exceeds 10 min (most real sessions: S268 ran 12m16s with single UP at 17:58:26) trigger guaranteed false-positive at next SessionStart. **20+ historical HIGH fires** May 10-13 in `.harness-health.log` — none documented in agent-notes/mistake-log. State=RED-1 became the new normal, defeating purpose of harness-health monitoring. AP-23 Forward-Policy auto-fix at 1st-instance (deterministic-script bug, not LLM-rule); fix: skip HH-2 entirely when `EVENT=SessionStart`, only run on `UserPromptSubmit` event (where a UP just happened and we verify it logged). Moved `EVENT=\"${1:-${CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT:-unknown}}\"` from line ~320 to line ~14 so HH2_check sees it. Companion firing-test: 2 new TCs (4c skip-on-SessionStart asserts SKIP marker + no FAIL; 4d regression-guard asserts HH-2 STILL fires on UserPromptSubmit when UP genuinely absent). 10/10 firing-tests PASS. Real-project verification: cleared `.harness-health-cache-unknown`; re-ran with SessionStart → state=GREEN skip=3; re-ran with UserPromptSubmit → state=GREEN skip=2 (HH-2 ran). Lesson meta: alarm fatigue is the failure mode that hides REAL failures — RED-1 ignored → if a real harness break occurred, would also be ignored. Charter Principle 11 honored. **PROMOTED-IN-S269** (in-place fix to existing hook; not new hook creation; joins L-S264-1 / L-S264-2 / L-S261-2 / L-S265-1 in deterministic-hook shipped lineage = 5 total). | SHIPPED-IN-S269 | high | inline-S269 |"," "," ---"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"163bc9ca-e6e3-4947-8b7a-e724602a2118","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"ff7c6871-e690-4fdf-bb47-521c4b06efa1","version":"2.1.138","gitBranch":"main"}

Set 0 to disable (rotation skipped).\n\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nJSONL_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nARCHIVE_DIR=\"$JSONL_DIR/.dispatch-pending-archive\"\nROTATION_THRESHOLD_HRS=\"${STOCKFORGE_DISPATCH_ROTATION_HRS:-12}\"\n\n# Kill switch: threshold=0 disables rotation entirely\nif [ \"$ROTATION_THRESHOLD_HRS\" = \"0\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Numeric validation\ncase \"$ROTATION_THRESHOLD_HRS\" in\n  ''|*[!0-9]*) ROTATION_THRESHOLD_HRS=12 ;;\nesac\n\nTHRESHOLD_SEC=$(( ROTATION_THRESHOLD_HRS * 3600 ))\nNOW=$(date +%s 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\nif [ \"$NOW\" -le 0 ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\nCUTOFF=$(( NOW - THRESHOLD_SEC ))\n\nif [ ! -d \"$JSONL_DIR\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\nROTATED=0\nROTATED_LIST=\"\"\nfor f in \"$JSONL_DIR\"/.dispatch-pending-*.jsonl; do\n  if [ ! -f \"$f\" ]; then\n    continue\n  fi\n  mtime=$(stat -c %Y \"$f\" 2>/dev/null || stat -f %m \"$f\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n  if [ \"$mtime\" -lt \"$CUTOFF\" ] 2>/dev/null; then\n    if [ ! -d \"$ARCHIVE_DIR\" ]; then\n      mkdir -p \"$ARCHIVE_DIR\" 2>/dev/null || continue\n    fi\n    bn=$(basename \"$f\")\n    if mv \"$f\" \"$ARCHIVE_DIR/$bn\" 2>/dev/null; then\n      ROTATED=$((ROTATED + 1))\n      ROTATED_LIST=\"$ROTATED_LIST $bn\"\n    fi\n  fi\ndone\n\nif [ \"$ROTATED\" -gt 0 ]; then\n  printf 'INFO: dispatch-pending-rotation archived %s stale ledger(s) > %sh to %s\\n' \\\n    \"$ROTATED\" \"$ROTATION_THRESHOLD_HRS\" \"$ARCHIVE_DIR\" >&2\nfi\n\nexit 0\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":802,"cache_read_input_tokens":172463,"output_tokens":1794,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":802,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[{"input_tokens":1,"output_tokens":1794,"cache_read_input_tokens":172463,"cache_creation_input_tokens":802,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":802},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011Cb3yPatqAH3NCLWjH38hX","type":"assistant","uuid":"6d0735b9-8752-4e21-9e61-37ec41584762","timestamp":"2026-05-15T07:37:33.330Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"ff7c6871-e690-4fdf-bb47-521c4b06efa1","version":"2.1.138","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"1951fc7b-72f6-4f70-a341-92755f39af92","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_013GvzGKmBYy4VL3H7tQuvd5","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01U3DDFo94uw4LD9pBi6Ue9S","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\firing-tests\\dispatch-pending-rotation-fire-test.sh","content":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# dispatch-pending-rotation-fire-test.sh — companion firing-test per L-S51-1.\n# Tests externally-observable behavior per L-S59-1:\n#   - stale ledgers (mtime > threshold) → moved to .dispatch-pending-archive/\n#   - fresh ledgers (mtime <= threshold) → untouched\n#   - empty directory / no ledgers → exit 0 clean\n#   - archive dir auto-created\n#   - STOCKFORGE_DISPATCH_ROTATION_HRS override honored\n#   - kill switch (HRS=0) disables rotation\n# L-S62-1: no yes|head patterns; fixtures via printf.\nset -uo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nHOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../dispatch-pending-rotation.sh\"\nif [ ! -f \"$HOOK\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL: hook not found at $HOOK\"\n  exit 1\nfi\n\nTEMPDIR=\"$(mktemp -d)\"\ntrap 'rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR\"' EXIT\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/proj\"\nJSONL_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nARCHIVE_DIR=\"$JSONL_DIR/.dispatch-pending-archive\"\nmkdir -p \"$JSONL_DIR\"\n\nPASS=0\nFAIL=0\n\nmake_pending() {\n  local sid=\"$1\"\n  local mtime_offset_hrs=\"$2\"\n  local f=\"$JSONL_DIR/.dispatch-pending-${sid}.jsonl\"\n  printf '{\"dispatch_id\":\"toolu_test\",\"tool_use_id\":\"toolu_test\",\"agent_type\":\"sandwich-dev\",\"model\":\"sonnet\",\"parent_session_id\":\"%s\",\"ts_ms\":1000000000000,\"state\":\"pending\"}\\n' \"$sid\" > \"$f\"\n  # Set mtime to NOW - offset_hrs\n  local now\n  now=$(date +%s)\n  local target=$(( now - mtime_offset_hrs * 3600 ))\n  # touch -d takes epoch via @ on GNU; fall back to -t YYYYMMDDhhmm on BSD\n  touch -d \"@$target\" \"$f\" 2>/dev/null || touch -t \"$(date -d \"@$target\" +%Y%m%d%H%M.%S 2>/dev/null || echo 200001010000)\" \"$f\" 2>/dev/null || true\n  printf '%s' \"$f\"\n}\n\nclean_state() {\n  rm -rf \"$JSONL_DIR\"/.dispatch-pending-*.jsonl\n  rm -rf \"$ARCHIVE_DIR\"\n}\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n# TC1 — stale ledger (mtime 24h ago) with default threshold 12h → archived\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\nclean_state\nF1=\"$(make_pending \"sess-tc1-stale\" 24)\"\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" >/dev/null 2>&1\nRC=$?\n\nif [ \"$RC\" -eq 0 ] \\\n   && [ ! -f \"$F1\" ] \\\n   && [ -f \"$ARCHIVE_DIR/.dispatch-pending-sess-tc1-stale.jsonl\" ]; then\n  echo \"PASS TC1: stale ledger (24h old) → archived under default 12h threshold\"\n  PASS=$((PASS+1))\nelse\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: expected archive of stale ledger; rc=$RC orig_exists=$([ -f \"$F1\" ] && echo y || echo n) archive_exists=$([ -f \"$ARCHIVE_DIR/.dispatch-pending-sess-tc1-stale.jsonl\" ] && echo y || echo n)\"\n  FAIL=$((FAIL+1))\nfi\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n# TC2 — fresh ledger (mtime 1h ago) → untouched\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\nclean_state\nF2=\"$(make_pending \"sess-tc2-fresh\" 1)\"\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" >/dev/null 2>&1\nRC=$?\n\nif [ \"$RC\" -eq 0 ] \\\n   && [ -f \"$F2\" ] \\\n   && [ ! -f \"$ARCHIVE_DIR/.dispatch-pending-sess-tc2-fresh.jsonl\" ]; then\n  echo \"PASS TC2: fresh ledger (1h old) → untouched, no archive\"\n  PASS=$((PASS+1))\nelse\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: fresh ledger should NOT be archived; rc=$RC orig_exists=$([ -f \"$F2\" ] && echo y || echo n)\"\n  FAIL=$((FAIL+1))\nfi\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n# TC3 — no ledgers present → exit 0 clean\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\nclean_state\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" >/dev/null 2>&1\nRC=$?\n\nif [ \"$RC\" -eq 0 ]; then\n  echo \"PASS TC3: no ledgers → exit 0 (no-op)\"\n  PASS=$((PASS+1))\nelse\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: expected exit 0, got $RC\"\n  FAIL=$((FAIL+1))\nfi\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n# TC4 — env override STOCKFORGE_DISPATCH_ROTATION_HRS=2 promotes 4h-old ledger to stale\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\nclean_state\nF4=\"$(make_pending \"sess-tc4-override\" 4)\"\nSTOCKFORGE_DISPATCH_ROTATION_HRS=2 CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" >/dev/null 2>&1\nRC=$?\n\nif [ \"$RC\" -eq 0 ] \\\n   && [ ! -f \"$F4\" ] \\\n   && [ -f \"$ARCHIVE_DIR/.dispatch-pending-sess-tc4-override.jsonl\" ]; then\n  echo \"PASS TC4: env override (HRS=2) archives 4h-old ledger\"\n  PASS=$((PASS+1))\nelse\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: env override should archive 4h ledger when HRS=2; rc=$RC orig_exists=$([ -f \"$F4\" ] && echo y || echo n)\"\n  FAIL=$((FAIL+1))\nfi\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n# TC5 — kill switch STOCKFORGE_DISPATCH_ROTATION_HRS=0 disables rotation entirely\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\nclean_state\nF5=\"$(make_pending \"sess-tc5-killed\" 999)\"  # Very stale\nSTOCKFORGE_DISPATCH_ROTATION_HRS=0 CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" >/dev/null 2>&1\nRC=$?\n\nif [ \"$RC\" -eq 0 ] \\\n   && [ -f \"$F5\" ] \\\n   && [ ! -d \"$ARCHIVE_DIR\" ]; then\n  echo \"PASS TC5: kill switch (HRS=0) disables rotation; super-stale file untouched\"\n  PASS=$((PASS+1))\nelse\n  echo \"FAIL TC5: kill switch should preserve ledger; rc=$RC orig_exists=$([ -f \"$F5\" ] && echo y || echo n) archive_dir=$([ -d \"$ARCHIVE_DIR\" ] && echo y || echo n)\"\n  FAIL=$((FAIL+1))\nfi\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n# TC6 — mixed (fresh + stale) → only stale archived; fresh untouched\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\nclean_state\nF6_FRESH=\"$(make_pending \"sess-tc6-fresh\" 1)\"\nF6_STALE=\"$(make_pending \"sess-tc6-stale\" 48)\"\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" >/dev/null 2>&1\nRC=$?\n\nif [ \"$RC\" -eq 0 ] \\\n   && [ -f \"$F6_FRESH\" ] \\\n   && [ ! -f \"$F6_STALE\" ] \\\n   && [ -f \"$ARCHIVE_DIR/.dispatch-pending-sess-tc6-stale.jsonl\" ] \\\n   && [ ! -f \"$ARCHIVE_DIR/.dispatch-pending-sess-tc6-fresh.jsonl\" ]; then\n  echo \"PASS TC6: mixed batch → only stale archived; fresh untouched\"\n  PASS=$((PASS+1))\nelse\n  echo \"FAIL TC6: mixed batch handling; rc=$RC\"\n  ls \"$JSONL_DIR\" \"$ARCHIVE_DIR\" 2>/dev/null\n  FAIL=$((FAIL+1))\nfi\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n# TC7 — idempotency: 2nd run after archive completes is no-op\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\nclean_state\nF7=\"$(make_pending \"sess-tc7-idem\" 24)\"\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" >/dev/null 2>&1\n# 2nd run\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" >/dev/null 2>&1\nRC=$?\n\nif [ \"$RC\" -eq 0 ] \\\n   && [ -f \"$ARCHIVE_DIR/.dispatch-pending-sess-tc7-idem.jsonl\" ] \\\n   && [ ! -f \"$F7\" ]; then\n  echo \"PASS TC7: idempotent — 2nd run is no-op (archive preserved, no duplicate)\"\n  PASS=$((PASS+1))\nelse\n  echo \"FAIL TC7: idempotency; rc=$RC\"\n  FAIL=$((FAIL+1))\nfi\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n# TC8 — no false-positive on hook itself when project dir missing\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"/nonexistent/path/abc123\" bash \"$HOOK\" >/dev/null 2>&1\nRC=$?\n\nif [ \"$RC\" -eq 0 ]; then\n  echo \"PASS TC8: missing project dir → exit 0 (graceful)\"\n  PASS=$((PASS+1))\nelse\n  echo \"FAIL TC8: expected exit 0 on missing project dir, got $RC\"\n  FAIL=$((FAIL+1))\nfi\n\necho \"\"\necho \"=== Results: PASS=$PASS FAIL=$FAIL (8 TCs) ===\"\n[ \"$FAIL\" -eq 0 ] && exit 0 || exit 1\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1865,"cache_read_input_tokens":173265,"output_tokens":4081,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1865,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[{"input_tokens":1,"output_tokens":4081,"cache_read_input_tokens":173265,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1865,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1865},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011Cb3yVEbcKwYLycoPGvtyd","type":"assistant","uuid":"f269f9c6-6cbc-4524-9b66-f950f8ad6996","timestamp":"2026-05-15T07:39:07.816Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"ff7c6871-e690-4fdf-bb47-521c4b06efa1","version":"2.1.138","gitBranch":"main"}

-f \"$HOOK\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL: hook not found at $HOOK\"\n  exit 1\nfi\n\nTEMPDIR=\"$(mktemp -d)\"\ntrap 'rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR\"' EXIT\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/proj\"\nJSONL_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nARCHIVE_DIR=\"$JSONL_DIR/.dispatch-pending-archive\"\nmkdir -p \"$JSONL_DIR\"\n\nPASS=0\nFAIL=0\n\nmake_pending() {\n  local sid=\"$1\"\n  local mtime_offset_hrs=\"$2\"\n  local f=\"$JSONL_DIR/.dispatch-pending-${sid}.jsonl\"\n  printf '{\"dispatch_id\":\"toolu_test\",\"tool_use_id\":\"toolu_test\",\"agent_type\":\"sandwich-dev\",\"model\":\"sonnet\",\"parent_session_id\":\"%s\",\"ts_ms\":1000000000000,\"state\":\"pending\"}\\n' \"$sid\" > \"$f\"\n  # Set mtime to NOW - offset_hrs\n  local now\n  now=$(date +%s)\n  local target=$(( now - mtime_offset_hrs * 3600 ))\n  # touch -d takes epoch via @ on GNU; fall back to -t YYYYMMDDhhmm on BSD\n  touch -d \"@$target\" \"$f\" 2>/dev/null || touch -t \"$(date -d \"@$target\" +%Y%m%d%H%M.%S 2>/dev/null || echo 200001010000)\" \"$f\" 2>/dev/null || true\n  printf '%s' \"$f\"\n}\n\nclean_state() {\n  rm -rf \"$JSONL_DIR\"/.dispatch-pending-*.jsonl\n  rm -rf \"$ARCHIVE_DIR\"\n}\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n# TC1 — stale ledger (mtime 24h ago) with default threshold 12h → archived\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\nclean_state\nF1=\"$(make_pending \"sess-tc1-stale\" 24)\"\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" >/dev/null 2>&1\nRC=$?\n\nif [ \"$RC\" -eq 0 ] \\\n   && [ ! -f \"$F1\" ] \\\n   && [ -f \"$ARCHIVE_DIR/.dispatch-pending-sess-tc1-stale.jsonl\" ]; then\n  echo \"PASS TC1: stale ledger (24h old) → archived under default 12h threshold\"\n  PASS=$((PASS+1))\nelse\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: expected archive of stale ledger; rc=$RC orig_exists=$([ -f \"$F1\" ] && echo y || echo n) archive_exists=$([ -f \"$ARCHIVE_DIR/.dispatch-pending-sess-tc1-stale.jsonl\" ] && echo y || echo n)\"\n  FAIL=$((FAIL+1))\nfi\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n# TC2 — fresh ledger (mtime 1h ago) → untouched\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\nclean_state\nF2=\"$(make_pending \"sess-tc2-fresh\" 1)\"\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" >/dev/null 2>&1\nRC=$?\n\nif [ \"$RC\" -eq 0 ] \\\n   && [ -f \"$F2\" ] \\\n   && [ ! -f \"$ARCHIVE_DIR/.dispatch-pending-sess-tc2-fresh.jsonl\" ]; then\n  echo \"PASS TC2: fresh ledger (1h old) → untouched, no archive\"\n  PASS=$((PASS+1))\nelse\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: fresh ledger should NOT be archived; rc=$RC orig_exists=$([ -f \"$F2\" ] && echo y || echo n)\"\n  FAIL=$((FAIL+1))\nfi\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n# TC3 — no ledgers present → exit 0 clean\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\nclean_state\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" >/dev/null 2>&1\nRC=$?\n\nif [ \"$RC\" -eq 0 ]; then\n  echo \"PASS TC3: no ledgers → exit 0 (no-op)\"\n  PASS=$((PASS+1))\nelse\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: expected exit 0, got $RC\"\n  FAIL=$((FAIL+1))\nfi\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n# TC4 — env override STOCKFORGE_DISPATCH_ROTATION_HRS=2 promotes 4h-old ledger to stale\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\nclean_state\nF4=\"$(make_pending \"sess-tc4-override\" 4)\"\nSTOCKFORGE_DISPATCH_ROTATION_HRS=2 CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" >/dev/null 2>&1\nRC=$?\n\nif [ \"$RC\" -eq 0 ] \\\n   && [ ! -f \"$F4\" ] \\\n   && [ -f \"$ARCHIVE_DIR/.dispatch-pending-sess-tc4-override.jsonl\" ]; then\n  echo \"PASS TC4: env override (HRS=2) archives 4h-old ledger\"\n  PASS=$((PASS+1))\nelse\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: env override should archive 4h ledger when HRS=2; rc=$RC orig_exists=$([ -f \"$F4\" ] && echo y || echo n)\"\n  FAIL=$((FAIL+1))\nfi\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n# TC5 — kill switch STOCKFORGE_DISPATCH_ROTATION_HRS=0 disables rotation entirely\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\nclean_state\nF5=\"$(make_pending \"sess-tc5-killed\" 999)\"  # Very stale\nSTOCKFORGE_DISPATCH_ROTATION_HRS=0 CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" >/dev/null 2>&1\nRC=$?\n\nif [ \"$RC\" -eq 0 ] \\\n   && [ -f \"$F5\" ] \\\n   && [ ! -d \"$ARCHIVE_DIR\" ]; then\n  echo \"PASS TC5: kill switch (HRS=0) disables rotation; super-stale file untouched\"\n  PASS=$((PASS+1))\nelse\n  echo \"FAIL TC5: kill switch should preserve ledger; rc=$RC orig_exists=$([ -f \"$F5\" ] && echo y || echo n) archive_dir=$([ -d \"$ARCHIVE_DIR\" ] && echo y || echo n)\"\n  FAIL=$((FAIL+1))\nfi\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n# TC6 — mixed (fresh + stale) → only stale archived; fresh untouched\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\nclean_state\nF6_FRESH=\"$(make_pending \"sess-tc6-fresh\" 1)\"\nF6_STALE=\"$(make_pending \"sess-tc6-stale\" 48)\"\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" >/dev/null 2>&1\nRC=$?\n\nif [ \"$RC\" -eq 0 ] \\\n   && [ -f \"$F6_FRESH\" ] \\\n   && [ ! -f \"$F6_STALE\" ] \\\n   && [ -f \"$ARCHIVE_DIR/.dispatch-pending-sess-tc6-stale.jsonl\" ] \\\n   && [ ! -f \"$ARCHIVE_DIR/.dispatch-pending-sess-tc6-fresh.jsonl\" ]; then\n  echo \"PASS TC6: mixed batch → only stale archived; fresh untouched\"\n  PASS=$((PASS+1))\nelse\n  echo \"FAIL TC6: mixed batch handling; rc=$RC\"\n  ls \"$JSONL_DIR\" \"$ARCHIVE_DIR\" 2>/dev/null\n  FAIL=$((FAIL+1))\nfi\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n# TC7 — idempotency: 2nd run after archive completes is no-op\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\nclean_state\nF7=\"$(make_pending \"sess-tc7-idem\" 24)\"\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" >/dev/null 2>&1\n# 2nd run\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" >/dev/null 2>&1\nRC=$?\n\nif [ \"$RC\" -eq 0 ] \\\n   && [ -f \"$ARCHIVE_DIR/.dispatch-pending-sess-tc7-idem.jsonl\" ] \\\n   && [ ! -f \"$F7\" ]; then\n  echo \"PASS TC7: idempotent — 2nd run is no-op (archive preserved, no duplicate)\"\n  PASS=$((PASS+1))\nelse\n  echo \"FAIL TC7: idempotency; rc=$RC\"\n  FAIL=$((FAIL+1))\nfi\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n# TC8 — no false-positive on hook itself when project dir missing\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"/nonexistent/path/abc123\" bash \"$HOOK\" >/dev/null 2>&1\nRC=$?\n\nif [ \"$RC\" -eq 0 ]; then\n  echo \"PASS TC8: missing project dir → exit 0 (graceful)\"\n  PASS=$((PASS+1))\nelse\n  echo \"FAIL TC8: expected exit 0 on missing project dir, got $RC\"\n  FAIL=$((FAIL+1))\nfi\n\necho \"\"\necho \"=== Results: PASS=$PASS FAIL=$FAIL (8 TCs) ===\"\n[ \"$FAIL\" -eq 0 ] && exit 0 || exit 1\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"f269f9c6-6cbc-4524-9b66-f950f8ad6996","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"ff7c6871-e690-4fdf-bb47-521c4b06efa1","version":"2.1.138","gitBranch":"main"}
